## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EU_ELECTRONIC_COMMUNICATIONS_CODE_CURRENT__2026_DIGITAL_NETWORKS_ACT_IS_PROPOSAL_NOT_OPERATIVE_REPLACEMENT__SPECTRUM_LICENSING_UNIVERSAL_SERVICE_AND_NATIONAL_IMPLEMENTATION_DYNAMIC"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The EECC remains the operative EU telecom baseline. The Commission adopted a Digital Networks Act proposal in January 2026, so BUILD02 locks PROPOSAL != OPERATIVE REPLACEMENT; spectrum, licensing and universal-service questions still require the competent jurisdiction.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Digital Networks Act**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/digital-networks-act
  - Verified point: The Commission adopted the Digital Networks Act proposal in January 2026; proposal status is not an operative replacement of the EECC.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 COMMUNICATION_TRUST_IDENTITY_INFRASTRUCTURE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-086-TELECOMMUNICATIONS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "電信法"
  english_name: "Telecommunications Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "誰可以建設電子通信網路、提供通信服務、取得 spectrum / numbering / rights of way、與其他 network interconnect；國家如何在競爭、universal service、reliability、emergency communications、privacy、cybersecurity、lawful interception 與國際 ITU obligations 之間分配稀缺通信資源與公共責任？"

  historical_reason:
    "通信網路具有強烈 network effects、sunk cost 與 bottleneck 特性；無線通信又依賴稀缺 spectrum，跨境干擾不能由單一國家獨立解決。傳統 monopoly utility 因技術與競爭自由化逐漸轉成 licensing、interconnection、spectrum management、SMP / competition remedies、universal service 與 consumer protection。現代電信法還必須處理 satellites、IoT、emergency networks、cybersecurity 與平台／內容分界。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "scarce spectrum vs market entry"
    - "investment incentives vs access / interconnection"
    - "network autonomy vs interoperability"
    - "universal service vs competition neutrality"
    - "licence certainty vs technology neutrality"
    - "privacy vs lawful interception"
    - "national spectrum control vs international coordination"
    - "network resilience vs cost"
    - "incumbent infrastructure vs new entrant rights"
    - "consumer choice vs switching / lock-in"
    - "emergency prioritization vs equal treatment"
    - "telecom regulation vs content regulation"
    - "public safety vs network shutdown abuse"

  shortest_maxim:
    "電信法第一眼不是『這是一家網路公司』；先問『它提供哪一層 network / service、使用哪些 spectrum / numbering / infrastructure resources、需何種 authorization、與誰互連、是否具有 bottleneck / SMP、對 coverage、quality、privacy、security 負哪些義務』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 92.1 第一眼｜NETWORK / SERVICE → AUTHORIZATION → INFRASTRUCTURE → SPECTRUM → NUMBERING → INTERCONNECTION → MARKET POWER → UNIVERSAL SERVICE → USER RIGHTS → SECURITY / INTERCEPTION → OUTAGE → REVIEW

```text
NETWORK / SERVICE
＝fixed？mobile？satellite？broadband？IoT？wholesale？

AUTHORIZATION
＝general authorization？individual licence？registration？

INFRASTRUCTURE
＝tower？fiber？submarine cable？rights of way？

SPECTRUM
＝allocation？assignment？licence？shared/unlicensed band？

NUMBERING
＝number block？portability？emergency number？

INTERCONNECTION
＝termination？roaming？wholesale access？

MARKET POWER
＝dominance / SMP？bottleneck？

UNIVERSAL SERVICE
＝coverage？affordability？accessibility？

USER RIGHTS
＝contract？quality？switching？privacy？

SECURITY / INTERCEPTION
＝cybersecurity？lawful authority？

OUTAGE
＝technical event？licence breach？force majeure？

REVIEW
＝regulator appeal？competition authority？court？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 92.32 證據特徵

- telecom licence；
- spectrum assignment；
- frequency plan；
- ITU filing / coordination；
- numbering allocation；
- interconnection agreement；
- wholesale offer；
- market analysis；
- universal-service order；
- coverage map；
- QoS measurements；
- tower / fiber permits；
- traffic / outage logs；
- cybersecurity incident；
- lawful-interception order；
- subscriber contract；
- tariff / billing；
- regulator decision；
- appeal / judicial review。

核心：

> **電信案件不能被「有牌照」或「有頻譜」一句關掉；真正要追的是服務、頻率、號碼、網路節點與監管義務是不是在同一時點彼此對得上。**

## 92.34 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依 service、resource、country、date 現查：

1. telecom regulatory authority；
2. network / service authorization；
3. spectrum allocation；
4. spectrum assignment / auction；
5. licence term / renewal；
6. spectrum sharing / trading；
7. interference；
8. ITU RR 2024 exact provision / effective date；
9. satellite / orbital filing；
10. numbering / portability；
11. rights of way；
12. tower / infrastructure sharing；
13. interconnection；
14. roaming；
15. wholesale access；
16. dominance / SMP；
17. competition remedies；
18. universal service；
19. accessibility；
20. QoS；
21. consumer contract / billing；
22. emergency communications；
23. network shutdown orders；
24. lawful interception；
25. data retention / privacy；
26. cybersecurity；
27. outage reporting；
28. submarine cables；
29. ITU-T standards incorporation；
30. ITR treaty status；
31. sanctions / equipment restrictions；
32. appeals / judicial review。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "EIDAS_TRUST_SERVICES_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT_AS_AMENDED_BY_EUDI__IMPLEMENTING_STANDARDS_AND_NATIONAL_SUPERVISION_DYNAMIC"
```

The eIDAS trust-services framework remains current as amended by the European Digital Identity framework. Technical standards, qualified-provider status, supervision and cross-border use remain dynamic and cannot be inferred from an electronic-signature label alone.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — European Digital Identity Regulation**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/eudi-regulation
  - Verified point: The amended European Digital Identity framework is in force and Member States are to provide EU Digital Identity Wallets by the end of 2026.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 COMMUNICATION_TRUST_IDENTITY_INFRASTRUCTURE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-113-ELECTRONIC_SIGNATURES_TRUST_SERVICES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "電子簽章／信任服務法"
  english_name: "Electronic Signatures / Trust Services Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某個electronic method是否完成signature、seal、timestamp、registered delivery、archiving、website authentication或electronic ledger等trust function；誰建立、憑什麼certificate / credential、用什麼reliable method、data是否完整、credential是否有效／撤銷；該trust output究竟產生何種法律推定，又有哪些問題仍須另外證明？"

  historical_reason:
    "紙本世界用親筆簽名、印章、郵戳、公證時間與掛號回執分散承擔身份、意圖、完整性、時間與送達功能。電子世界沒有天然筆跡與紙張，因此需要把這些信賴功能拆解成不同trust services，並讓法律對可靠方法提供相應的recognition或presumption，而不是把一張『數位證書』變成萬能真實印璽。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "technology neutrality vs assurance levels"
    - "convenience vs signer control"
    - "certificate trust vs credential compromise"
    - "cross-border recognition vs local qualification"
    - "signature intent vs authentication mechanics"
    - "origin/integrity vs truth of content"
    - "timestamp certainty vs event truth"
    - "qualified status vs private-law defences"
    - "legal-person seal vs natural-person intent"
    - "long-term validation vs algorithm obsolescence"
    - "revocation speed vs reliance"
    - "trust lists vs real-time operational status"

  shortest_maxim:
    "電子簽章法第一眼不是『有沒有數位憑證』；先問『這個trust mechanism到底要證明哪一件事：誰簽、哪個法人出處、資料有沒有被改、什麼時間存在、何時送達？不要讓一個certificate替五個不同命題一起作證』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 119.1 第一眼｜TRUST FUNCTION → ACTOR → CREDENTIAL / CERTIFICATE → SIGNED / SEALED DATA → METHOD → VALIDATION → TIME → REVOCATION → RELIANCE → LEGAL EFFECT / PRESUMPTION

```text
TRUST FUNCTION
＝signature? seal? timestamp? delivery? archive? website auth?

ACTOR
＝natural signatory? legal person? trust provider?

CREDENTIAL
＝certificate? key? wallet credential?

SIGNED DATA
＝which exact hash / document version?

METHOD
＝simple? advanced? qualified? reliable method?

VALIDATION
＝certificate path? trust list? algorithm?

TIME
＝signing time? trusted timestamp?

REVOCATION
＝valid then? revoked now?

RELIANCE
＝who checked what?

LEGAL EFFECT
＝equivalence? presumption? evidence only?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 119.26 Evidence architecture

- exact signed data/hash；
- signature format；
- certificate；
- chain；
- trust list；
- signing device；
- validation report；
- revocation status；
- timestamp；
- seal；
- delivery receipt；
- archival record；
- website certificate；
- trust-provider status；
- compromise logs；
- authority evidence；
- applicable e-sign law；
- eIDAS qualification where relevant。

核心：

> **trust service的每一項輸出都必須只證明它有資格證明的那一小段：身份、來源、完整性、時間或送達。越是「qualified」，越不能把它誤用成整份文件真實性的王冠。**

```yaml
  domain_core_question:
    "一個identity-management system如何把physical/legal person與digital credential連結；relying party究竟需要知道full identity、某個attribute、credential possession還是authorization；identity proofing、authentication、wallet、attribute attestation、revocation、recovery、cross-border recognition、privacy與liability如何形成信賴鏈？"

  historical_reason:
    "面對面交易靠長相、證件、印章與機構人員確認身份；網路交易中，帳號與肉身分離，credential可以被複製、盜用、代理或遠端控制。數位身分法因此不是問『帳號叫什麼』，而是建立一條從初次identity proofing、credential issuance、authentication、attribute presentation到revocation/recovery的生命週期，並讓relying party只取得完成交易所需的身份確信。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "identity assurance vs privacy"
    - "full identification vs selective disclosure"
    - "security vs accessibility"
    - "centralised identity vs user control"
    - "biometrics vs irreversibility"
    - "cross-border recognition vs national identity sovereignty"
    - "wallet convenience vs device compromise"
    - "relying-party assurance vs data minimisation"
    - "fraud prevention vs exclusion"
    - "account recovery vs impersonation risk"
    - "government credentials vs voluntary use"
    - "age assurance vs anonymity"
    - "identity proof vs legal authority"

  shortest_maxim:
    "數位身分法第一眼不是『帳號是不是本人』；先問『這個服務其實需要知道本人完整身份，還是只需知道某個attribute；credential怎麼發、誰現在控制、是否已撤銷、驗證成功究竟證明到哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 120.1 第一眼｜IDENTITY SUBJECT → REQUIRED CLAIM → PROOFING → CREDENTIAL ISSUER → WALLET / HOLDER → AUTHENTICATION → ATTRIBUTE PRESENTATION → RELYING PARTY → AUTHORIZATION → LIFECYCLE / REVOCATION → REMEDY

```text
IDENTITY SUBJECT
＝natural person? legal person? representative?

REQUIRED CLAIM
＝name? age? licence? company role?

PROOFING
＝passport? registry? remote video? biometric?

CREDENTIAL ISSUER
＝state? bank? university? professional body?

WALLET / HOLDER
＝device? cloud? smart card?

AUTHENTICATION
＝password? MFA? passkey? biometric?

ATTRIBUTE PRESENTATION
＝full DOB? over-18 proof? qualification?

RELYING PARTY
＝merchant? government? bank?

AUTHORIZATION
＝is person allowed to act?

LIFECYCLE
＝expiry? revocation? device loss? recovery?

REMEDY
＝correction? challenge? liability?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 120.28 Evidence architecture

- proofing method；
- source documents；
- issuer；
- credential；
- assurance level；
- wallet/device；
- authentication logs；
- biometric score/liveness；
- attribute presentation；
- relying-party request；
- consent / data minimisation；
- revocation status；
- recovery event；
- device loss；
- delegation / authority；
- MLIT enactment；
- eIDAS/EUDI current national implementation。

核心：

> **數位身分案不能只問「系統說這是誰」。要追到：最初誰證明了這個身份、用什麼證據、發了什麼credential、現在誰控制它、relying party究竟需要相信哪一個最小命題。**

以下必須依identity ecosystem、subject、jurisdiction、date現查：

26. cross-border recognition；

The EU Data Act is applicable from 12 September 2025, alongside the wider EU data-governance framework. This does not override GDPR, sector access rules, confidentiality, trade secrets or later legislative amendments.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Data Act**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/news-and-media/news/data-act-enters-force-what-it-means-you-2024-01-11_en
  - Verified point: The Data Act became applicable on 12 September 2025; sector, personal-data and later amendment questions remain separate.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-11／INT-12 的 PARTIAL_SUPERSESSION_STRUCTURAL 決定，數位／資料法保留共同概念、入口與 router 法位；具體專門卡在自身 first move 取得優先，來源母卡不得被刪除，也不得重新擴張成專門卡的第二份 authoritative body。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-014-DIGITAL_DATA_LAW"
  chinese_name: "數位／資料法"
  english_name: "Digital / Data Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "資料可被持續蒐集、分析、複製、結合、推論、移轉與跨境使用時，誰可以基於什麼法律資格處理哪些資料、為了什麼目的、持續多久、與誰分享，以及個人與組織如何取得存取、更正、反對、安全與救濟的位置？"

  historical_reason:
    "數位資料不像傳統物品只能由一人占有；同一資料可以被多方同時複製、分析並產生新的推論。單純使用『所有權』概念很難回答隱私、個資、資料安全、跨境流動、演算法推論與資料共享。因此現代資料法更常透過處理目的、法律基礎、角色、透明、權利、安全與責任配置來治理。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "資料利用／創新 vs 隱私與人格自主"
    - "跨境資料流 vs 國家監管與資料主權"
    - "個人控制 vs 組織必要處理"
    - "透明度 vs 商業秘密／資安"
    - "資料最小化 vs AI／分析對大量資料的需求"
    - "個人同意 vs 權力不對等與同意疲勞"
    - "資料可攜與競爭 vs 安全與第三人權利"
    - "去識別化利用 vs 再識別風險"
    - "自動化決策效率 vs 可解釋、異議與公平"

  shortest_maxim:
    "資料法第一眼不是『資料屬於誰』；先問『這是什麼資料、誰在處理、基於什麼資格、為了什麼目的、流向哪裡、對誰產生什麼效果』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 20.1 第一眼｜DATA → ACTOR → PROCESSING → BASIS → PURPOSE → FLOW → EFFECT

先拆：

```text
DATA
＝個人資料？敏感資料？匿名／去識別？企業資料？公共資料？機密？

ACTOR
＝資料主體？控制者？處理者？共同控制者？資料持有人？接收者？

PROCESSING
＝蒐集？儲存？分析？推論？分享？出售？訓練？監控？刪除？

LEGAL BASIS / AUTHORITY
＝同意？契約？法律義務？公共任務？合法利益？其他法定基礎？

PURPOSE
＝為什麼處理？

FLOW
＝傳給誰？跨境？第三方？政府？

EFFECT
＝廣告？信用？招聘？保險？內容推薦？執法？自動化決策？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 20.11 證據特徵

資料案件的證據常是系統生命週期：

- 隱私政策；
- consent logs；
- data inventory；
- data flow map；
- processing records；
- API logs；
- access logs；
- retention settings；
- deletion records；
- vendor contracts；
- DPA；
- cross-border transfer mechanism；
- security architecture；
- incident logs；
- breach notification；
- model training dataset record；
- profiling logic；
- automated decision logs；
- DPIA／risk assessment；
- regulator communications。

核心：

> **資料法不是只看一張隱私政策；要追資料實際怎麼流。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "GDPR_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__ADEQUACY_DECISIONS_AND_NATIONAL_ENFORCEMENT_DYNAMIC__AI_BIOMETRIC_CHILDREN_AND_PUBLIC_AUTHORITY_OVERLAYS_REQUIRED"
```

GDPR remains the baseline EU personal-data framework. Adequacy decisions and national enforcement are live status questions; biometric, children, AI and public-authority processing require additional legal bases and overlays.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Adequacy decisions**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/law/law-topic/data-protection/international-dimension-data-protection/adequacy-decisions_en
  - Verified point: Adequacy decisions are an official and changeable transfer-status list; transfer legality cannot be frozen generically.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 DATA_PROCESSING_GOVERNANCE_FLOW 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-133-PRIVACY_PERSONAL_DATA_PROTECTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "隱私／個人資料保護法"
  english_name: "Privacy / Personal Data Protection Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某資訊是否與identified / identifiable natural person具有足夠關聯而落入適用個資制度；誰決定processing purposes and means、誰受託處理、誰受處理影響；每一處理活動依何種法律基礎、為何種特定目的、是否必要／比例／透明、保存多久、如何保障安全與個人權利；涉及special-category data、children、profiling、automated decisions、government access或高風險處理時，又有哪些加重資格與救濟？"

  historical_reason:
    "數位經濟使個人可被裝置識別碼、行為軌跡、位置、關係網、購買紀錄、健康資料與推論模型持續描述。單靠傳統『秘密空間不受侵擾』的隱私概念，無法處理資料被合法取得後的再利用、推論、連結與自動化決策；但若把所有資料關係改寫成個人完整所有權，又會忽略契約履行、公共任務、醫療、反詐與其他合法處理需要。現代個資法因此把問題重組為角色、合法基礎、目的、資料品質、權利、安全、責任與監督的制度鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先畫真實 data flow 與 processing purpose；不要先問『有沒有勾同意』，也不要把隱私政策文字當成系統實際行為。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "informational self-determination vs necessary social / commercial processing"
    - "consent autonomy vs consent fatigue / power imbalance"
    - "purpose limitation vs secondary use / innovation"
    - "data minimisation vs analytics / AI training demand"
    - "transparency vs complexity / trade secrets / security"
    - "data subject rights vs third-party rights and operational integrity"
    - "profiling efficiency vs discrimination / autonomy / due process"
    - "anonymisation utility vs re-identification risk"
    - "public security / government access vs privacy and legality"
    - "global services vs territorially fragmented privacy law"
    - "accountability documentation vs paper compliance"
    - "privacy by design vs legacy systems"

  shortest_maxim:
    "個資法第一眼不是『這是誰的資料』；先問『這是不是受保護的personal data、誰以什麼角色做了哪個processing、基於什麼資格與目的、資料實際流到哪裡、對人產生什麼效果、哪些權利與責任因此被觸發』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-54.1 第一眼｜DATA → IDENTIFIABILITY → ACTOR → PROCESSING → LEGAL BASIS → PURPOSE → NECESSITY → RIGHTS → SECURITY → ACCOUNTABILITY → REMEDY

```text
DATA
＝direct identifier? device ID? location? health? inference? behavioural profile?

IDENTIFIABILITY
＝identified / identifiable under applicable law?

ACTOR
＝data subject? controller? processor? joint controller? recipient? authority?

PROCESSING
＝collect? record? infer? combine? disclose? train? monitor? delete?

LEGAL BASIS / AUTHORITY
＝consent? contract? legal obligation? vital interests? public task? legitimate interests? special statute?

PURPOSE
＝what exact purpose was specified and actually pursued?

NECESSITY / PROPORTIONALITY
＝is this data / use / retention needed for that purpose?

RIGHTS
＝notice? access? correction? deletion? objection? restriction? portability? human review?

SECURITY
＝confidentiality? integrity? availability? incident handling?

ACCOUNTABILITY
＝records? DPIA? governance? vendor control? DPO / responsible officer where required?

REMEDY
＝complaint? order? deletion? damages? administrative fine? judicial review?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-54.22 Evidence architecture

- privacy notices by version；
- consent / preference logs；
- records of processing；
- system architecture / data inventory；
- data lineage / flow map；
- controller-processor contracts；
- access / audit logs；
- retention schedules；
- deletion jobs；
- DPIA / risk assessments；
- model / profiling documentation；
- data subject request records；
- breach tickets / notifications；
- regulator correspondence；
- actual database / API evidence；
- third-party SDK and recipient list。

核心：

> **個資案件不能只審文件說系統應該怎麼流；要證明資料實際怎麼流、誰在什麼時點做了什麼決定。**

以下必須依法域與時間現查：

1. personal data / identifiable person definition；

## FAILURE_MODES

## G2-54.23 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "personal data＝individual owns the data as absolute property。"
  - "consent＝all processing permanently lawful。"
  - "no name＝anonymous。"
  - "public data＝privacy law never applies。"
  - "privacy policy＝actual processing proof。"
  - "processor label＝processor legal status。"
  - "AI inference＝not personal data。"
  - "parent consent＝all child-data use lawful。"
  - "data breach＝only public exfiltration。"
  - "data subject right＝unlimited disclosure / deletion。"
  - "Convention 108+ already in force in 2026。"
```

The Data Act is current from 12 September 2025 and creates data-access, sharing and cloud-switching rules within scope. Data-holder identity, sector rules, personal-data constraints and interoperability duties still require case classification.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Data Act**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/news-and-media/news/data-act-enters-force-what-it-means-you-2024-01-11_en
  - Verified point: The Data Act became applicable on 12 September 2025; sector, personal-data and later amendment questions remain separate.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 DATA_PROCESSING_GOVERNANCE_FLOW 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-134-DATA_GOVERNANCE_ACCESS_SHARING_LAW"
  chinese_name: "資料治理／資料存取與共享法"
  english_name: "Data Governance / Data Access & Sharing Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一組personal或non-personal data由誰持有、控制、生成或合法取得；哪些主體基於契約、法定access right、portability、public-sector reuse、research、data intermediary、data space或public-interest need取得存取／分享／再利用資格；資料可用性如何與privacy、trade secrets、IP、competition、security、confidentiality、interoperability及公平契約條件協調？"

  historical_reason:
    "資料的社會與經濟價值常在再利用、組合與共享中產生，但『能複製』不等於『任何人有權拿』。物聯網、平台、研究、公共資料與產業資料空間使資料權益不再只存在於個資法：產品使用者可能需要存取自己設備產生的資料，維修商需要經授權取得資料，公共部門持有的受限制資料可能以安全條件再利用，企業之間又可能因市場力量而形成不公平的data-access條款。資料治理法因此處理的不是單一所有權，而是access、control、sharing、interoperability、stewardship與safeguards的組合制度。"

  first_move:
    "先辨資料種類、holder／user／recipient與access source；不要從『資料是我產生的』或『資料在我伺服器』直接推出所有權與排他權。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "data access / innovation vs privacy / confidentiality"
    - "holder control vs user-generated value"
    - "mandatory sharing vs freedom of contract"
    - "interoperability vs security / IP / trade secrets"
    - "open data vs protected / restricted data"
    - "public-interest reuse vs purpose / rights constraints"
    - "data portability vs third-party rights"
    - "competition / contestability vs investment incentives"
    - "data altruism / research vs governance and trust"
    - "cloud switching vs technical lock-in"
    - "data value allocation vs ownership simplification"
    - "AI training demand vs lawful source / access rights"

  shortest_maxim:
    "資料治理第一眼不是『誰擁有data』；先問『這是什麼資料、誰持有或生成、誰依哪個法源／契約有權access或要求sharing、資料要用來做什麼、有哪些privacy／trade-secret／security／competition邊界，以及共享後誰繼續負責』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-55.1 第一眼｜DATA TYPE → HOLDER → ACCESS CLAIMANT → SOURCE OF RIGHT → PURPOSE → SHARING MECHANISM → SAFEGUARDS → INTEROPERABILITY → VALUE / COMPENSATION → REMEDY

```text
DATA TYPE
＝personal? non-personal? mixed? public-sector? IoT? industrial? research? confidential?

HOLDER / CONTROL POSITION
＝manufacturer? platform? user? public body? researcher? cloud provider?

ACCESS CLAIMANT
＝individual? business user? third-party service? competitor? authority? researcher?

SOURCE OF RIGHT
＝contract? statutory right? portability? open-data law? sector rule? public-interest request?

PURPOSE
＝repair? analytics? research? switching? public service? model training?

SHARING MECHANISM
＝API? export? secure room? data intermediary? data space? direct download?

SAFEGUARDS
＝privacy? trade secret? security? confidentiality? IP? purpose controls?

INTEROPERABILITY
＝format / API / metadata / machine readability / semantic compatibility?

VALUE / COMPENSATION
＝free? reasonable compensation? FRAND-like rule? negotiated fee?

REMEDY
＝access order? contract claim? regulator? competition remedy? injunction?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-55.20 Evidence architecture

- data inventory / taxonomy；
- source / generation record；
- product telemetry specifications；
- access terms / licences；
- API specifications；
- export formats；
- user / third-party requests；
- data-sharing agreements；
- confidentiality / trade-secret measures；
- public-sector reuse decision；
- interoperability documentation；
- cloud switching records；
- compensation / fee calculation；
- refusal reason；
- security controls；
- regulator decisions。

核心：

> **資料共享案件先證明「誰要求什麼資料、憑什麼要求、目前誰能控制、技術上怎麼交付、交付會碰到哪些第三人權利」，不要先把data ownership當答案。**

4. data intermediaries / data altruism；
5. B2B mandatory sharing；
6. compensation / unfair contractual terms；
7. interoperability / cloud switching；
8. trade-secret safeguards；
9. personal-data overlay；
10. database / IP rights；
11. competition / platform-specific data access；
12. research / health / mobility / energy sector data rules；
13. AI training / text-and-data mining interface；
14. Data Act / DGA temporal application；
15. regulator powers and remedies。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "GDPR_TRANSFER_TOOLKIT_CURRENT__ADEQUACY_AND_TRANSFER_MECHANISMS_DYNAMIC__LOCALIZATION_GOVERNMENT_ACCESS_AND_TRADE_OVERLAYS_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Cross-border data transfer remains mechanism- and destination-specific. Adequacy status is dynamic and other transfer mechanisms require their own conditions; localization, public-authority access and trade rules cannot be collapsed into a single transfer label.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Adequacy decisions**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/law/law-topic/data-protection/international-dimension-data-protection/adequacy-decisions_en
  - Verified point: Adequacy decisions are an official and changeable transfer-status list; transfer legality cannot be frozen generically.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 DATA_PROCESSING_GOVERNANCE_FLOW 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-135-CROSS_BORDER_DATA_TRANSFER_DATA_LOCALIZATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "跨境資料移轉／資料在地化法"
  english_name: "Cross-Border Data Transfer / Data Localization Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "哪些資料、哪個exporter / importer / remote accessor、哪一個來源法域與目的法域構成受規範的cross-border transfer或境外存取；適用制度要求adequacy、certification、standard clauses、binding corporate rules、consent／specific derogation、government approval、security assessment、local storage／local copy、sector restriction或其他mechanism中的哪一種；onward transfer、foreign-government access、enforcement、data sovereignty與trade commitments又如何改變合法性？"

  historical_reason:
    "雲端與跨國企業讓資料在技術上可以瞬間跨境，但法律上的『出境』不只等於把硬碟搬過海關：遠端管理、全球support、跨國資料湖、第三國subprocessor與政府命令都可能讓資料進入另一法律秩序。各國一方面希望資料自由流動支撐數位貿易、研究與全球服務，另一方面擔心隱私、國安、執法可得性、監管落空與資料主權。跨境資料法因此不是『出國可不可以』的單一開關，而是transfer trigger、mechanism、destination protection、government access、localization與onward-flow治理的複合資格鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先畫 source jurisdiction → exporter → recipient / remote accessor → destination → onward recipients；不要只看資料中心地點，也不要把『使用海外雲端』直接等同違法。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "free flow of data vs privacy / fundamental rights"
    - "global cloud efficiency vs regulatory reach"
    - "data localization vs security / resilience / trade cost"
    - "private contractual safeguards vs foreign government access"
    - "adequacy recognition vs sovereign regulatory differences"
    - "enterprise global operations vs onward-transfer control"
    - "law-enforcement access vs international comity / due process"
    - "national security vs commercial predictability"
    - "digital trade commitments vs legitimate public-policy restrictions"
    - "centralized global data lake vs sectoral localization"
    - "certification / clauses vs actual technical practice"
    - "transfer compliance vs data-access law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "跨境資料第一眼不是『資料在哪台server』；先問『哪個法域把哪個行為定義成transfer／境外存取、誰把什麼資料交給誰、依哪個mechanism、目的地法律與政府存取風險如何、是否有localization／sector rule、後續再轉移由誰控制』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-56.1 第一眼｜SOURCE LAW → DATA → TRANSFER TRIGGER → EXPORTER → IMPORTER → DESTINATION → MECHANISM → GOVERNMENT ACCESS → ONWARD TRANSFER → LOCALIZATION → REMEDY

```text
SOURCE LAW
＝which jurisdiction's transfer restriction applies?

DATA
＝personal? sensitive? health? financial? critical / industrial? non-personal?

TRIGGER
＝physical transfer? remote access? cloud storage? onward sharing? support access?

EXPORTER
＝controller? processor? public body? sector entity?

IMPORTER
＝affiliate? vendor? cloud provider? independent controller? authority?

DESTINATION
＝country / territory / sector / international organisation?

MECHANISM
＝adequacy? SCC? BCR? certification? contract? approval? derogation?

GOVERNMENT ACCESS
＝foreign law compels access? redress? proportionality? conflict of laws?

ONWARD TRANSFER
＝can importer send again? to whom? under what safeguard?

LOCALIZATION
＝store locally? local copy? processing ban? approval? sector restriction?

REMEDY
＝suspend transfer? order localization? fine? injunction? contract remedy?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-56.24 Evidence architecture

- data-flow map by jurisdiction；
- data-centre / region configuration；
- remote support locations；
- controller / processor / importer roles；
- adequacy decision and exact scope；
- SCC / BCR / certification documents；
- transfer impact / risk assessments where applicable；
- subprocessor list；
- onward-transfer terms；
- encryption / key-management architecture；
- government request policy / transparency reports；
- local law memo；
- localization statute / sector regulator rule；
- regulator approval / notification；
- retention / deletion after transfer；
- incident / government-demand records。

核心：

> **跨境資料合規不是保存一份SCC，而是能把每一條實際資料路徑、接收者、法源、目的地風險與onward chain重新畫出來。**

18. enforcement and remedies。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-058-MEDIA_LAW"
  chinese_name: "媒體法"
  english_name: "Media Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "在新聞、廣播、電視、公共媒體、社區媒體與數位傳播共同形成公共資訊環境時，國家可以在哪些範圍規範所有權、頻譜、牌照、透明度、公共責任與安全；又如何避免監管本身變成控制新聞內容、排除異議或把公共資訊入口交給少數權力中心？"

  historical_reason:
    "新聞媒體不只是一般企業：它能調查權力、設定議題、形成公共記憶，也受市場、所有權、廣告與國家資訊控制影響。廣播時代的頻譜稀缺促成許可與公共義務；數位時代則使分發權部分移向搜尋、社群與推薦平台。媒體法因此同時處理 press freedom、independence、pluralism、ownership、licensing、public service media、journalist safety、source protection、access to information、platform distribution 與市場可存續性。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "新聞自由 vs 名譽／隱私／公平審判"
    - "編輯獨立 vs 所有權／資金控制"
    - "媒體多元 vs 市場集中"
    - "公共媒體責任 vs 政府控制"
    - "廣電牌照 vs viewpoint neutrality"
    - "新聞來源保護 vs 刑事司法／國安"
    - "記者安全 vs 公共秩序"
    - "資訊公開 vs 國家秘密／個資"
    - "新聞可存續性 vs 廣告／平台依賴"
    - "平台分發權 vs 編輯可見性"
    - "更正／答辯權 vs 編輯自主"
    - "媒體監管 vs censorship risk"

  shortest_maxim:
    "媒體法第一眼不是『這篇報導對不對』；先問『誰控制媒體與分發入口、這是什麼媒體制度、誰能規範、誰能影響編輯、資訊與資金從哪裡進來、公共多元性如何被保護』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 64.1 第一眼｜MEDIUM → OWNER → EDITORIAL CONTROL → FUNDING → DISTRIBUTION → REGULATOR → CONTENT / PROCESS DUTY → SAFEGUARD

```text
MEDIUM
＝print？broadcast？public service？community？online newsroom？podcast？

OWNER
＝誰實質控制？

EDITORIAL CONTROL
＝誰決定採訪、標題、刊登、撤稿？

FUNDING
＝subscription？advertising？state budget？grant？donor？

DISTRIBUTION
＝頻道？frequency？cable？search？social platform？app store？

REGULATOR
＝一般法院？media regulator？telecom regulator？competition authority？

DUTY
＝licensing？ownership disclosure？public-service remit？correction？safety？

SAFEGUARD
＝independence？appeal？transparent criteria？judicial review？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 64.22 證據特徵

- ownership register；
- beneficial ownership；
- licence；
- funding；
- state advertising allocation；
- editorial policy；
- regulator order；
- journalist accreditation；
- source-protection order；
- search warrant；
- newsroom records；
- access-to-information request；
- correction / reply demand；
- platform ranking / monetization evidence；
- AI workflow；
- safety threat reports；
- market / audience data。

核心：

> **媒體法的證據不只看某一篇稿件，而要看誰控制資訊生產、分配與生存條件。**

The DSA generally applies from 17 February 2024, while platform designation and enforcement status remain dynamic. Illegal-content classification, defamation, incitement and constitutional/human-rights analysis remain dependent on governing law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Digital Services Act Q&A**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/faqs/digital-services-act-questions-and-answers
  - Verified point: The DSA generally applies from 17 February 2024; VLOP/VLOSE designation and enforcement status remain dynamic.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 MEDIA_EXPRESSION_PUBLIC_INFORMATION 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-059-JOURNALISM_FREEDOM_OF_EXPRESSION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "新聞／言論法"
  english_name: "Journalism / Freedom of Expression Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項意見、事實主張、新聞報導、評論、影像或其他表達受到哪個表意自由法源保護；若國家或私人主張名譽、隱私、國安、公平審判、公共秩序、仇恨煽動或其他權利受侵害，應以什麼明確規則區分不受歡迎的言論、可合法限制的言論與必須禁止的煽動？"

  historical_reason:
    "公共討論必然包含錯誤、衝突、批評、冒犯與權力揭露。如果國家只保護正確、溫和或官方認可的言論，表意自由便失去制度功能；但表達也可能侵害名譽、隱私、審判、公民平等或造成直接暴力風險。新聞／言論法因此以 opinion / expression 區分、Article 19(3) limitation test、defamation、privacy、incitement、prior restraint 與有效救濟建立界線。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "表意自由 vs 名譽"
    - "新聞報導 vs 隱私"
    - "政治批評 vs 國家權威"
    - "公開司法 vs fair trial"
    - "令人冒犯言論 vs public order"
    - "錯誤資訊 vs censorship"
    - "仇恨言論 vs criminal incitement threshold"
    - "匿名表達 vs accountability"
    - "來源／秘密 vs 國安"
    - "prior restraint vs post-publication liability"
    - "個人名譽救濟 vs chilling effect"
    - "平台私人 moderation vs state censorship"

  shortest_maxim:
    "新聞／言論法第一眼不是『這句話好不好』；先問『誰說、說了什麼、是意見還是可驗證事實、在什麼語境、對誰傳播、造成什麼法律利益衝突、誰想用什麼法源限制』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 65.1 第一眼｜SPEAKER → EXPRESSION → FORM → FACT / OPINION → AUDIENCE → CONTEXT → HARM CLAIM → LEGAL BASIS → TEST → REMEDY

```text
SPEAKER
＝citizen？journalist？public employee？politician？company？anonymous user？

EXPRESSION
＝text？speech？image？satire？symbol？algorithmic output？

FORM
＝news？opinion？academic？artistic？political？commercial？

FACT / OPINION
＝可證偽的 factual assertion？value judgment？mixed?

AUDIENCE
＝private？small group？public？viral？

CONTEXT
＝election？protest？war？court case？commercial dispute？

HARM CLAIM
＝defamation？privacy？incitement？threat？security？contempt？

LEGAL BASIS
＝哪條法律？

TEST
＝legality / legitimate aim / necessity / proportionality？

REMEDY
＝reply？correction？damages？injunction？criminal sanction？removal？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

- context；

- correction；

- privacy circumstances；
- threat evidence；
- hate-speech context；
- government takedown request；

> **言論案件最忌諱只截一句。必須保存完整表達、前後語境、speaker、audience、時間與實際傳播方式。**

# DLM-013｜智慧財產法

```yaml
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 19｜DLM-013｜智慧財產法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B05"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "D:組織／企業／市場、F:財產／交易／債與交換"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-11／INT-12 的 PARTIAL_SUPERSESSION_STRUCTURAL 決定，智慧財產法保留共同概念、入口與 router 法位；具體專門卡在自身 first move 取得優先，來源母卡不得被刪除，也不得重新擴張成專門卡的第二份 authoritative body。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-013-INTELLECTUAL_PROPERTY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "智慧財產法"
  english_name: "Intellectual Property Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "創作、發明、品牌、設計與機密資訊具有高複製性與低邊際複製成本時，法律為什麼在一定期間、範圍與條件內賦予排他權；又如何防止排他權反過來封鎖公共知識、競爭、表達與後續創新？"

  historical_reason:
    "知識與資訊一旦公開，往往可以被低成本重製與使用；若創作者、發明人與企業無法在一定範圍內回收投入，可能降低創作與創新誘因。但過強、過久或錯置的排他權又會增加學習、競爭與後續創作成本。因此智慧財產法不是一般物權的複製，而是一組以特定客體、有限期間、地域性與例外限制構成的制度性排他權。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "創新／創作誘因 vs 知識與文化流通"
    - "排他權 vs 後續創新與競爭"
    - "作者／發明人利益 vs 公眾使用"
    - "商標識別保護 vs 語言與市場自由"
    - "秘密保護 vs 勞動流動與合法逆向工程"
    - "地域性權利 vs 全球數位流通"
    - "權利確定性 vs 新科技、新型利用方式"
    - "民事排他權 vs 言論、競爭與基本權"

  shortest_maxim:
    "智慧財產第一眼不是『誰先想到』；先問『保護的是哪一種客體、權利怎麼取得、保護到哪裡、對方做了哪一個受控制行為、又有沒有例外或失效』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 19.1 第一眼｜SUBJECT MATTER → RIGHT → OWNERSHIP → ACT → LIMITATION → REMEDY

任何智慧財產爭議先拆：

```text
SUBJECT MATTER
＝著作？發明？商標？設計？營業秘密？地理標示？其他法定客體？

RIGHT
＝權利是自動產生、註冊取得、審查授予，還是靠秘密狀態維持？

OWNERSHIP
＝誰是原始權利人？有無僱傭、委託、讓與、授權？

CONTROLLED ACT
＝重製？公開傳輸？製造？使用？販售？進口？標示？揭露？不正取得？

LIMITATION
＝合理使用／法定例外／權利用盡／先前使用／強制授權／競爭限制？

REMEDY
＝禁制？損害賠償？返還？銷毀？邊境措施？刑事／行政？
```

沒有先辨客體，就不能直接說：

> 「這是我的智慧財產。」

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 19.9 證據特徵

常見材料：

- 創作草稿／版本；
- Git／原始碼紀錄；
- 發明筆記；
- 專利申請文件；
- 商標註冊；
- 使用證據；
- 授權／讓與；
- 僱傭／委託契約；
- NDA；
- 存取權限；
- 保密措施；
- 被控產品／程式／作品；
- 銷售資料；
- 行銷；
- 原始檔；
- 專家鑑定；
- 先前技術；
- 網頁存證；
- 交易／下載／使用紀錄。

核心：

> **IP 案件的證據常同時證明「權利存在」與「對方行為落入權利範圍」，兩件事不能合併。**

## 19.11 Positive Law Gate

以下必須現查：

1. 權利類型與法定客體；
2. 權利取得方式；
3. 保護期間；
4. 地域；
5. 原始歸屬；

7. 授權與讓與；

11. 營業秘密構成與保密措施；
12. 權利用盡；
13. 邊境措施；
14. 民事／刑事／行政救濟；
15. WIPO／WTO 條約適用與國內實施；
16. AI 訓練、生成內容、資料庫等新型利用問題。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-127-COPYRIGHT_RELATED_RIGHTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "著作權／鄰接權法"
  english_name: "Copyright / Related Rights Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一具體內容是否構成受保護的作品／表達或受鄰接權保護的表演、錄音、廣播等客體；誰在何地、何時取得作者／權利人法位；被控行為是否構成重製、改作、散布、公開演出／傳輸／向公眾提供或其他受控制利用；又是否有期限、授權、例外、法定許可、權利用盡、公共領域或其他抗辯限制該權利？"

  historical_reason:
    "創作一旦可被印刷、錄音、攝影、廣播、數位複製與網路傳輸，作品可以在幾乎不消耗原件的情況下大量擴散。著作權因此不是保護『想法本身』，而是在一定期間內對具體表達及若干相鄰產業成果配置排他權，同時藉由例外、限制、公共領域與期限避免知識、教育、評論、文化再創作與技術使用被永久封閉。數位環境又使一次使用可能同時觸發重製、傳輸、平台、資料與跨境問題，因此第一眼必須先把作品、權利、行為與例外分開。"

  first_move:
    "先辨認受保護客體與被控利用行為，不先問『兩個作品像不像』，也不先把『我先創作』直接翻成『我擁有所有利用權』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "author / right-holder incentive vs public access / public domain"
    - "expression protection vs idea / fact / method freedom"
    - "automatic protection vs evidentiary certainty"
    - "economic rights vs moral rights"
    - "exclusive rights vs exceptions / limitations"
    - "territorial rights vs borderless digital dissemination"
    - "individual licensing vs collective management"
    - "technological protection vs lawful access / exceptions"
    - "creator ownership vs employer / commissioner / assignee interests"
    - "human authorship concepts vs AI-assisted / AI-generated production"
    - "copyright vs related rights"
    - "enforcement vs freedom of expression / competition / innovation"

  shortest_maxim:
    "著作權第一眼不是『很像所以侵權』；先問『哪個受保護表達、誰有權、對方做了哪個受控制行為、用了多少與什麼實質部分、又有沒有授權、例外或公共領域資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-34.1 第一眼｜WORK / SUBJECT MATTER → AUTHORSHIP → OWNERSHIP → RIGHT → ACT → SUBSTANTIALITY / CAUSAL LINK → LICENSE / EXCEPTION → TERM → REMEDY

```text
WORK / SUBJECT MATTER
＝literary? artistic? musical? audiovisual? software? database compilation? photograph? architecture?

AUTHORSHIP
＝誰創作？是否符合該法域作者資格？共同作者？職務創作？AI-assisted?

OWNERSHIP
＝作者本人？雇主？受讓人？委託關係？集體管理？

RIGHT
＝reproduction? adaptation? distribution? performance? communication/making available? rental?

ACT
＝copy? upload? stream? train? cache? translate? sample? perform? sell?

SUBSTANTIALITY / CAUSAL LINK
＝是否實際接觸／複製？利用的是受保護表達還是idea/fact/style/function？

LICENSE / EXCEPTION
＝express / implied licence? statutory exception? fair use/fair dealing? quotation? education? accessibility?

TERM
＝仍在保護期？public domain?

REMEDY
＝injunction? damages? account? statutory damages where available? border / criminal remedy?
```

最小鏈：

```text
PROTECTED SUBJECT MATTER
+
QUALIFIED RIGHT HOLDER
+
CONTROLLED ACT
+
LEGALLY RELEVANT COPYING / USE
-
LICENSE / EXCEPTION / EXPIRY
=
INFRINGEMENT CANDIDATE
```

任何一段都可以獨立失敗。

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-34.23 Evidence architecture

- original files / drafts / timestamps；
- source-control history；
- publication records；
- metadata；
- registration / deposit where relevant；
- employment / commission agreement；
- assignment；
- licence；
- CMO mandate；
- allegedly copied work；
- access evidence；
- side-by-side expression comparison；
- upload / download / server logs；
- platform notices；
- takedown / counter-notice；
- TPM / DRM logs；
- revenue / audience / damages records。

核心：

> **著作權案件最危險的證據跳躍，是從「相似」直接跳到「複製了受保護表達」。中間至少還有作品資格、接觸／因果、保護範圍、實質利用與例外。**

12. distribution；

15. rental / lending；
16. software protection；
17. database protection；
18. related rights；
19. exceptions / fair use / fair dealing；
20. educational / research / quotation exceptions；
21. accessibility exceptions；
22. temporary copies；
23. TPM / RMI；
24. exhaustion / first sale；
25. term；
26. registration / recordation effects；
27. statutory damages where any；

29. notice-and-takedown regime；
30. AI training / text-data mining；

33. criminal / border enforcement。

Patent law remains jurisdiction-specific. The 2024 WIPO GRATK Treaty may affect future patent-disclosure architecture but is not yet in force, so it cannot be treated as a current universal patent rule.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **WIPO Lex — Treaty on Intellectual Property, Genetic Resources and Associated Traditional Knowledge status**
  - URL: https://www.wipo.int/wipolex/en/treaties/ShowResults?search_what=N&treaty_id=19830

- **WIPO — GRATK Treaty FAQs**
  - URL: https://www.wipo.int/export/sites/www/tk/en/docs/faqs-gratk-treaty.pdf
  - Verified point: Entry into force requires 15 instruments of ratification or accession and occurs three months after the fifteenth instrument.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 KNOWLEDGE_SIGNAL_EXCLUSIVITY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-128-PATENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "專利法"
  english_name: "Patent Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項技術方案是否屬於可專利客體並滿足新穎性、進步性／非顯而易見性、產業利用性／實用性與充分揭露等法定要求；誰具有inventor／applicant／owner法位、優先權與申請程序是否成立；專利核准後其有效claim應如何解釋，而被控產品、方法或使用是否逐項落入claim或法定等同範圍；又是否存在例外、權利用盡、強制授權、政府使用或其他限制？"

  historical_reason:
    "發明往往需要高額研發成本，但技術資訊一旦公開又可能被快速模仿。專利制度以公開換取一定期間的排他權：申請人必須揭露足以支持權利範圍的技術內容，國家則在新穎、進步與可利用等資格成立時授予限期權利。這使專利與著作權不同：專利通常依申請、審查與territorial grant成立，真正的爭點也不是『兩個產品很像』，而是有效claim的每一必要要件是否被被控技術實施。"

  first_move:
    "先拿到exact patent family、jurisdiction、priority chain與目前有效claims，再談侵權；不要從產品外觀或專利證書存在直接推出勝負。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "innovation incentive vs public access / competition"
    - "exclusive right vs disclosure bargain"
    - "broad claims vs enablement / support"
    - "first-to-file procedure vs true inventorship"
    - "territorial grant vs global technology market"
    - "patent certainty vs evolving claim interpretation"
    - "pharmaceutical incentive vs health access"
    - "standard-essential patents vs interoperability / competition"
    - "private exclusion vs compulsory licence / government use"
    - "early filing vs sufficiently complete disclosure"
    - "patentability of software / biotech / AI-related inventions vs exclusions"
    - "grant presumption / administrative decision vs later invalidity challenge"

  shortest_maxim:
    "專利法第一眼不是『他做了類似產品』；先問『哪個法域、哪件仍有效的patent、哪個claim、如何解釋、每一要件是否落入、而那個claim本身又能不能活過invalidity審查』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-35.1 第一眼｜JURISDICTION → PATENT FAMILY → PRIORITY → STATUS → CLAIM → CONSTRUCTION → ACCUSED EMBODIMENT → ELEMENT-BY-ELEMENT → DEFENCE / INVALIDITY → REMEDY

```text
JURISDICTION
＝which national / regional patent right?

PATENT FAMILY
＝which application / continuation / divisional / regional member?

PRIORITY
＝earliest valid priority? Paris priority? PCT route?

STATUS
＝pending? granted? expired? lapsed? revoked? opposed?

CLAIM
＝independent / dependent claim text?

CONSTRUCTION
＝what do claim terms mean in this jurisdiction?

ACCUSED EMBODIMENT
＝product? process? import? use? offer? supply of means?

ELEMENT-BY-ELEMENT
＝every limitation met?

DEFENCE / INVALIDITY
＝prior art? non-enablement? excluded subject matter? licence? exhaustion? exception?

REMEDY
＝injunction? damages? reasonable royalty? border / customs? validity proceedings?
```

核心：

```text
PATENT CERTIFICATE
≠ VALID CLAIM
≠ INFRINGED CLAIM
≠ ENTITLEMENT TO EVERY REMEDY
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-35.25 Evidence architecture

- patent number / jurisdiction；
- prosecution file；
- priority applications；
- assignment / ownership register；
- maintenance-fee status；
- current claims；
- claim chart；
- accused product teardown；
- source code / process documents；
- import / sales records；
- prior art publications；
- lab notebooks / invention records；
- expert reports；
- standards documents；
- licences / FRAND declarations；
- regulatory filings；
- damages / sales / royalty data。

核心：

> **專利案必須可以畫成 claim chart；如果分析還停在『兩邊技術很像』，通常還沒有進到專利侵權的法律層。**

### CURRENT-LAW GATE RESULT

- No single transnational source was allowed to masquerade as the governing domestic law. Runtime must resolve jurisdiction, date, object/system/resource classification and local positive law before merits closure.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-129-TRADEMARK_LAW"
  chinese_name: "商標法"
  english_name: "Trademark Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一文字、圖形、聲音、形狀、顏色、位置或其他標識在特定法域與商品／服務上是否具有可註冊／可保護資格與識別來源功能；權利是由註冊、使用、聲譽或其他法定路徑取得與維持；被控標識在實際市場使用下是否造成混淆、聯想、淡化、冒用、假冒或其他受規範效果；又是否存在描述性使用、比較廣告、先使用、權利用盡、撤銷、不使用或其他限制？"

  historical_reason:
    "市場交易需要讓消費者知道商品或服務來自誰。當品牌、包裝與標識可以被模仿，交易成本與品質訊號會被破壞；商標制度因此保護來源識別、商譽與市場秩序。但商標不是對一個字或顏色取得脫離商品、市場與territory的所有權。權利範圍會隨識別性、指定商品／服務、實際使用、聲譽、混淆與法定限制而變化，並且可能因不使用、通用化或無效而縮減或消失。"

  first_move:
    "先確認exact mark、territory、owner、registration/use basis與goods/services，再做市場情境比較；不要因兩個logo看起來像就直接宣布侵權。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "source identification vs free descriptive language"
    - "brand investment vs market entry / competition"
    - "registration certainty vs use-based reality"
    - "territorial rights vs global online branding"
    - "consumer confusion protection vs comparative / referential use"
    - "strong marks vs monopoly over ordinary words"
    - "famous-mark protection vs over-expansion"
    - "quality control / licensing vs commercial flexibility"
    - "anti-counterfeiting enforcement vs legitimate parallel trade"
    - "traditional visible marks vs non-traditional marks"
    - "trademark rights vs domain names / platform handles / keywords"
    - "private brand rights vs geographical indications / public signs"

  shortest_maxim:
    "商標法第一眼不是『logo像不像』；先問『哪個mark在什麼法域、對哪些goods/services取得什麼強度的來源識別權，對方在什麼市場脈絡怎麼使用，真正會不會產生法定混淆、淡化或其他效果』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-36.1 第一眼｜SIGN → TERRITORY → BASIS OF RIGHT → GOODS / SERVICES → DISTINCTIVENESS → USE → COMPARATOR → CONFUSION / OTHER THEORY → DEFENCE → STATUS / REMEDY

```text
SIGN
＝word? logo? slogan? shape? sound? color? position? trade dress?

TERRITORY
＝which registration / use-based jurisdiction?

BASIS OF RIGHT
＝registration? use? well-known status? passing off / unfair competition?

GOODS / SERVICES
＝exact specification and actual market?

DISTINCTIVENESS
＝inherent? acquired? descriptive? generic?

USE
＝as a mark? nominative/reference? comparative? decorative? keyword?

COMPARATOR
＝similarity of signs + goods/services + channels + consumers?

THEORY
＝likelihood of confusion? dilution? counterfeiting? unfair competition?

DEFENCE
＝descriptive use? prior right? consent? exhaustion? non-trademark use?

STATUS
＝valid? renewed? non-use? cancelled? limited?
```

核心：

```text
REGISTERED MARK
+
RELEVANT GOODS / SERVICES / REPUTATION
+
LEGALLY RELEVANT USE
+
CONFUSION / DILUTION / OTHER REQUIRED EFFECT
-
DEFENCE / LIMIT
=
INFRINGEMENT CANDIDATE
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-36.26 Evidence architecture

- registration certificate / database extract；
- current status / renewal；
- application / priority record；
- goods/services specification；
- Nice classes；
- first-use evidence；
- advertising spend；
- sales / market share；
- consumer surveys；
- media coverage；
- packaging / screenshots；
- actual confusion evidence；
- channels / price / consumer sophistication；
- licence / assignment；
- non-use evidence；
- customs seizure records；
- domain / platform records；
- comparative advertising materials。

核心：

> **商標案件不是logo鑑賞。要把標識、指定商品／服務、實際市場、消費者、權利強度與被控使用放在同一張市場關係圖裡。**

以下必須依法域、mark type與日期現查：

1. registrable signs；
2. distinctiveness；
3. acquired distinctiveness；
4. genericness / descriptiveness；
5. functionality；
6. public-order / prohibited signs；
7. official emblems / Article 6ter issues；
8. registration vs use-based rights；
9. first use / priority；
10. Paris priority；
11. goods/services specification；
12. Nice classification version；
13. opposition；
14. cancellation / invalidity；
15. renewal；
16. non-use period / genuine use；
17. likelihood-of-confusion factors；
18. well-known / famous marks；
19. dilution / unfair advantage；
20. counterfeiting；
21. non-traditional marks；
22. trade dress；
23. descriptive / nominative / referential use；
24. comparative advertising；
25. licensing / quality control；
26. assignment / goodwill；
27. exhaustion / parallel import；
28. domain-name remedies；
29. online keyword / marketplace use；
30. intermediary / platform procedures；
31. customs / criminal enforcement；
32. territoriality / cross-border forum；
33. remedies / damages / injunctions。

### CURRENT-LAW GATE RESULT

- No single transnational source was allowed to masquerade as the governing domestic law. Runtime must resolve jurisdiction, date, object/system/resource classification and local positive law before merits closure.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-130-TRADE_SECRET_LAW"
  chinese_name: "營業秘密法"
  english_name: "Trade Secret Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "被主張保護的information是否已被足夠具體識別，是否在相關圈子中不為一般所知或不容易取得、是否因其秘密性具有商業價值、lawful controller是否採取在情況下合理的保密措施；被告又是否以違反honest commercial practices、保密義務、信賴、契約或其他法定義務的方式取得、使用或揭露該資訊，而非合法的independent development、reverse engineering、public-source acquisition或其他例外？"

  historical_reason:
    "企業競爭優勢不只存在於可公開換取排他權的patent、copyright或registered design，也存在於配方、製程、原始碼、客戶資料、價格模型、實驗結果、商業策略與know-how。此類資訊若必須公開才能受保護，就會摧毀其價值；但若只要主張『這是秘密』就能取得排他權，又會不當限制員工流動、獨立研發、reverse engineering與市場競爭。因此營業秘密法的核心不是替秘密本身封聖，而是建立一條『秘密資格＋合理自保＋不正取得／使用／揭露』的責任鏈。"

  first_move:
    "先要求把secret說清楚，再看它怎麼被保密；不要從『對方離職後做出類似產品』直接跳到竊取營業秘密。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "confidential business value vs market competition"
    - "secrecy vs evidentiary disclosure in litigation"
    - "employee mobility vs employer confidential assets"
    - "reasonable secrecy measures vs operational practicality"
    - "protection against misappropriation vs independent innovation"
    - "reverse engineering freedom vs contractual restriction"
    - "commercial secrecy vs whistleblowing / public interest"
    - "trade secret strategy vs patent disclosure strategy"
    - "cybersecurity controls vs legal secrecy qualification"
    - "cross-border leakage vs territorial remedies"
    - "information specificity vs overbroad litigation claims"
    - "injunction speed vs due process"

  shortest_maxim:
    "營業秘密案第一眼不是『資料很重要』；先問『秘密到底是哪一段資訊、為什麼因秘密而有價值、誰依法控制、採取了哪些合理保密措施、對方到底怎麼取得／使用／揭露，以及是否存在獨立開發、reverse engineering、公共來源或其他合法路徑』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-43.1 第一眼｜SECRET IDENTIFICATION → SECRECY → COMMERCIAL VALUE → REASONABLE STEPS → LAWFUL CONTROL → ACQUISITION / USE / DISCLOSURE → IMPROPRIETY → EXCEPTIONS / DEFENCES → CAUSATION → REMEDY

```text
SECRET IDENTIFICATION
＝formula? source code? customer segment? pricing logic? negative know-how? compilation?

SECRECY
＝not generally known / not readily accessible in relevant circles?

COMMERCIAL VALUE
＝value because secret, not merely value in general?

REASONABLE STEPS
＝NDA? access control? classification? encryption? need-to-know? exit process?

LAWFUL CONTROL
＝who lawfully controls the information? employer? licensor? joint venture?

ACQUISITION / USE / DISCLOSURE
＝what act actually occurred?

IMPROPRIETY
＝espionage? breach of confidence? inducement? unauthorized copying? dishonest practice?

DEFENCE / LAWFUL PATH
＝independent development? reverse engineering? public source? whistleblowing exception?

CAUSATION
＝did the alleged use cause the claimed competitive or monetary harm?

REMEDY
＝injunction? preservation? damages? unjust enrichment? confidentiality order?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-43.30 Evidence architecture

- exact secret identification；
- creation / development history；
- public-source comparison；
- access-control matrix；
- confidentiality agreements；
- policies / training；
- source-control / repository logs；
- download / USB / cloud logs；
- email / messaging；
- forensic images；
- employee departure timeline；
- competitor development records；
- reverse-engineering records；
- licensing / collaboration terms；
- incident reports；
- valuation / head-start evidence；
- protective-order materials。

核心：

> **營業秘密案不是「我有資料＋你做得很像」；它是一條必須從secret identity、secrecy、value、reasonable steps一路接到specific improper act與causation的證據鏈。**

Industrial-design protection remains jurisdiction-specific. WIPO current status material shows the Riyadh Design Law Treaty is not yet in force, so treaty adoption cannot be treated as present uniform design law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **WIPO — Riyadh Design Law Treaty status document**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 KNOWLEDGE_SIGNAL_EXCLUSIVITY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-131-INDUSTRIAL_DESIGN_DESIGN_RIGHTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "工業設計／設計權法"
  english_name: "Industrial Design / Design Rights Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "被主張的design是否以適用法承認的representation與product／article關係被具體界定，是否符合novelty、originality、individual character或其他取得／存續要件，是否主要由technical function決定或落入其他排除；權利是否有效、由誰持有、在哪個territory／term內存在；被控產品又是否依該法域的scope test形成copy、substantial copy、same overall impression或其他侵權結果？"

  historical_reason:
    "工業產品的競爭不只有功能與品牌。家具輪廓、車燈形狀、包裝、鞋面、圖示、介面與產品表面裝飾本身也可能具有市場價值。專利法若只保護技術解決方案，著作權又不一定適合所有工業量產外觀，於是各法域發展registered / unregistered design regimes，以較專門的方式處理『產品看起來怎樣』。但設計法不能反過來成為功能壟斷工具，因此representation、novelty與function boundary構成核心資格鎖。"

  first_move:
    "先看註冊／申請圖與被主張的外觀範圍，不先看產品功能、品牌知名度或『整體很像』的直覺。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "design aesthetics vs technical function"
    - "visual innovation vs cumulative design freedom"
    - "registration certainty vs unregistered protection"
    - "representation precision vs fair scope"
    - "individual design rights vs spare-parts competition"
    - "design protection vs copyright overlap"
    - "design protection vs trademark / trade dress"
    - "global filing convenience vs territorial substantive law"
    - "early disclosure / marketing vs novelty"
    - "short product cycles vs registration timing"
    - "digital / animated design vs article-based doctrines"
    - "right-holder exclusivity vs repair / interoperability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "設計權第一眼不是『產品外觀很像』；先問『真正被保護的是哪一組圖示外觀、附著在哪個產品／部分、何時公開與申請、哪個取得要件與功能排除適用、權利在哪裡有效，再用該法域真正的scope test比較被控設計』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-44.1 第一眼｜RIGHT TYPE → REPRESENTATION → PRODUCT / PART → FILING / DISCLOSURE → NOVELTY / ORIGINALITY / INDIVIDUAL CHARACTER → FUNCTIONALITY → OWNERSHIP → TERRITORY / TERM → SCOPE → INFRINGEMENT → EXCEPTIONS / REMEDY

```text
RIGHT TYPE
＝registered design? unregistered design? design patent? copyright overlap?

REPRESENTATION
＝drawings? photos? views? broken lines? disclaimer? animation sequence?

PRODUCT / PART
＝whole product? component? packaging? GUI? icon?

TIMING
＝first disclosure? priority? filing? publication?

VALIDITY
＝new/original/individual character under exact law?

FUNCTIONALITY
＝is appearance dictated essentially / solely by technical function?

OWNERSHIP
＝designer? employer? commissioner? assignee?

TERRITORY / TERM
＝where and when does protection exist?

SCOPE
＝copy / substantial copy / overall impression / jurisdiction-specific test?

EXCEPTION
＝private use? experimental? repair? citation? other statutory route?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-44.29 Evidence architecture

- official registration/application；
- all representations / views；
- disclaimers / descriptions；
- filing / priority record；
- publication history；
- prior art；
- design development sketches；
- ownership / employment / assignment；
- Hague designation / refusal status；
- product samples；
- side-by-side comparison；
- expert evidence where permitted；
- sales / import records；
- validity challenges；
- renewal / term records。

核心：

> **設計權的邊界首先畫在官方representation與適用法，而不是畫在產品名稱、品牌故事或『一眼看起來很像』的直覺裡。**

GI/appellation protection depends on product, registration system, treaty/regional framework and domestic enforcement. BUILD02 therefore keeps current status jurisdiction/product specific rather than inventing a universal GI code.

### CURRENT-LAW GATE RESULT

- No single transnational source was allowed to masquerade as the governing domestic law. Runtime must resolve jurisdiction, date, object/system/resource classification and local positive law before merits closure.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-132-GEOGRAPHICAL_INDICATIONS_APPELLATIONS_OF_ORIGIN_LAW"
  chinese_name: "地理標示／原產地名稱法"
  english_name: "Geographical Indications / Appellations of Origin Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某name／sign是否識別特定地理來源，產品是否具有因該來源而形成的quality、characteristic或reputation，並符合適用法對GI／AO／PDO／PGI或其他origin-linked designation的連結強度；該名稱透過sui generis、collective/certification mark、unfair competition或其他制度取得何種保護，誰符合product specification而有使用資格，第三人使用是否構成misleading use、usurpation、imitation、evocation、translation/transliteration或其他法定侵害，又如何處理genericness、homonymous indications、prior trademarks與跨境recognition？"

  historical_reason:
    "某些商品的價值不是單一企業發明，而是地域、氣候、土壤、人群技術、地方聲譽與長期生產規範共同累積。若任何外地生產者都可搭便車使用該地名，地域共同體的聲譽可能被稀釋；但若任何地名都能被某一群體排他控制，也會妨礙一般語言、正當產地描述、既有商標與市場競爭。因此GI法不是單純品牌法，而是『地理來源＋品質／特性／聲譽連結＋集體規格＋合法使用者群』的制度。"

  first_move:
    "先問這個名稱到底指向哪個地理區域、哪類產品、何種quality/characteristic/reputation與產地有何證據連結，再找它在目標法域究竟透過哪一種制度受保護；不要看到地名就直接叫GI。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "collective regional reputation vs open market language"
    - "origin-linked quality vs generic product names"
    - "producer community control vs individual trademark rights"
    - "traditional production specification vs innovation"
    - "local identity vs international trade"
    - "strong GI protection vs territorial recognition"
    - "prior trademarks vs later GI claims"
    - "homonymous indications vs consumer confusion"
    - "wine / spirits enhanced protection vs general GI baseline"
    - "consumer information vs producer exclusivity"
    - "administrative certification vs private enforcement"
    - "climate / territorial change vs fixed product specification"

  shortest_maxim:
    "地理標示案第一眼不是『名字裡有地名』；先問『它指哪裡、哪種產品、哪個品質／特性／聲譽因該地而成立、誰制定與遵守specification、目標法域用哪套制度承認，再問第三人的具體使用是否落入那套制度真正禁止的行為』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-45.1 第一眼｜INDICATION → TERRITORY → PRODUCT → LINK → PROTECTION MODALITY → SPECIFICATION → AUTHORIZED USERS → CONFLICTS → INFRINGING USE → EXCEPTIONS → ENFORCEMENT

```text
INDICATION
＝name? sign? traditional designation? transliteration?

TERRITORY
＝country? region? locality? trans-border area?

PRODUCT
＝wine? spirit? food? handicraft? industrial product?

LINK
＝quality? characteristic? reputation attributable to origin?

MODALITY
＝sui generis? certification mark? collective mark? unfair competition?

SPECIFICATION
＝raw materials? production zone? method? quality control?

AUTHORIZED USERS
＝which producers comply?

CONFLICTS
＝generic term? prior mark? homonymous GI? personal/company name?

USE
＝misleading? imitation? evocation? translation? comparative statement?

ENFORCEMENT
＝producer group? authority? trademark owner? customs?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-45.32 Evidence architecture

- protected name / exact spelling；
- geographic boundary map；
- product category；
- specification / code of practice；
- historical production evidence；
- quality / characteristic / reputation studies；
- natural / human factor evidence；
- registration / recognition record；
- Lisbon status / refusal / grant；
- authorized producer records；
- inspection / certification reports；
- prior trademark records；
- generic-use evidence；
- consumer surveys where relevant；
- accused labels / packaging / ads；
- sales channels；
- customs origin documents；
- translations / transliterations；
- coexistence agreements / treaty provisions。

核心：

> **GI案不是『我們這地方很有名』；要把地理邊界、產品、來源連結、集體規格、承認制度、合法使用者與被控使用一段一段接起來。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "WIPO_GRATK_TREATY_ADOPTED_2024_NOT_YET_IN_FORCE__4_RATIFICATION_OR_ACCESSION_NOTIFICATIONS_BY_2026_07_08_VS_15_REQUIRED__DOMESTIC_TK_TCE_ABS_AND_PATENT_DISCLOSURE_RULES_DYNAMIC"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The WIPO GRATK Treaty was adopted in 2024 but is not yet in force. WIPO status records four ratification/accession notifications through 8 July 2026 against the fifteen required for entry into force; domestic TK/TCE/ABS regimes remain separate.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **WIPO Lex — Treaty on Intellectual Property, Genetic Resources and Associated Traditional Knowledge status**
  - URL: https://www.wipo.int/wipolex/en/treaties/ShowResults?search_what=N&treaty_id=19830
  - Verified point: The treaty is not yet in force; WIPO status records four ratification/accession notifications through 8 July 2026.
- **WIPO — GRATK Treaty FAQs**
  - URL: https://www.wipo.int/export/sites/www/tk/en/docs/faqs-gratk-treaty.pdf
  - Verified point: Entry into force requires 15 instruments of ratification or accession and occurs three months after the fifteenth instrument.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 KNOWLEDGE_SIGNAL_EXCLUSIVITY 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-201-TRADITIONAL_KNOWLEDGE_TRADITIONAL_CULTURAL_EXPRESSIONS_GENETIC_RESOURCES_IP_LAW"
  chinese_name: "傳統知識／傳統文化表現與遺傳資源智慧財產法"
  english_name: "Traditional Knowledge / Traditional Cultural Expressions & Genetic-Resources IP Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "由indigenous people、local community或其他依法受保護community跨世代保存、發展與傳承的traditional knowledge / traditional cultural expressions，或與genetic resources相關的traditional knowledge，在patent、copyright、trademark、design、database、sui generis、ABS與cultural-heritage制度中如何被辨識、保護、防止錯誤授權／misappropriation；誰有authority提供access、attribution、disclosure、consent與benefit sharing，若知識早已部分公開、由多人／多社群共享或具有sacred/secret character又如何處理？"

  historical_reason:
    "傳統IP制度偏好可識別作者／發明人、固定創作時點、有限期間與可轉讓私權；traditional knowledge與TCE卻可能由社群跨世代共同演化，沒有單一作者，也可能已公開但仍具有文化規範、來源與利益分享要求。生技又會把genetic resources與associated TK帶進patent system。2024 WIPO GRATK Treaty第一次在WIPO treaty層處理GR/TK patent disclosure，2026 broader TK/TCE仍在IGC談判，更顯示此領域不能被一般copyright/patent或文化遺產一張卡安全吸收。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識community / holder / custodian與subject matter：TK、TCE、GR-associated TK還是一般文化素材；再找真正source regime與origin/provenance，區分access / use / IP application / commercialization / disclosure / attribution / benefit-sharing。不得因material在網路上找得到，就直接判定community沒有任何legal interest；也不得因community主張traditional就自動創造一項全球排他IP權。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "individual authorship model vs collective custodianship"
    - "public domain doctrine vs cultural protocol"
    - "innovation access vs misappropriation"
    - "patent transparency vs applicant burden"
    - "community consent vs internal plurality"
    - "secret/sacred knowledge vs disclosure"
    - "commercialization vs cultural integrity"
    - "benefit-sharing vs commodification"
    - "national sovereignty over genetic resources vs community rights"
    - "IP term limits vs intergenerational interests"
    - "copyright fixation vs living traditions"
    - "global IP harmonization vs sui generis diversity"

  shortest_maxim:
    "傳統知識第一眼不是『網路上查得到所以誰都能拿』，也不是『祖傳所以自動有全球版權』；先問『是哪個community、哪種知識／文化表現、哪個法源、來源與取得過程、這次做的是access、專利、複製、商業化還是揭露，法律要求什麼同意、歸屬與利益分享』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-62.1 第一眼｜COMMUNITY / HOLDER → SUBJECT MATTER → SOURCE / REGIME → ORIGIN / PROVENANCE → ACCESS → USE / IP ACT → DISCLOSURE / ATTRIBUTION → BENEFIT → MISAPPROPRIATION → REMEDY

```text
COMMUNITY / HOLDER
＝indigenous people? local community? traditional custodian? multiple communities?

SUBJECT MATTER
＝TK? TCE? folklore? medicinal knowledge? craft? music? symbol? GR-associated TK?

SOURCE / REGIME
＝domestic sui generis? patent? copyright? trademark? heritage? ABS? treaty?

ORIGIN / PROVENANCE
＝where / from whom was it obtained?

ACCESS
＝who permitted access? under what conditions?

USE / IP ACT
＝research? patent application? copying? recording? branding? AI training? commercialization?

DISCLOSURE / ATTRIBUTION
＝source? origin? community? inventor disclosure?

BENEFIT
＝monetary? non-monetary? shared research? attribution?

MISAPPROPRIATION
＝wrongful patent? unauthorized use? false association? secret disclosure?

REMEDY
＝reject/revoke? injunction? correction? attribution? benefit-sharing? compensation?
```

- community identity evidence；
- traditional authority / mandate；

- cultural protocol；

- patent application；
- disclosure statement；

- recording；

核心：


The EU AI Act reached a major applicability date on 2 August 2026 and Article 50 transparency duties are active within scope. High-risk, GPAI, enforcement and any future amendment/omnibus timeline must still be checked provision by provision.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — AI Act Article 50 transparency guidelines**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/library/guidelines-transparency-obligations-providers-and-deployers-ai-systems
  - Verified point: Article 50 transparency duties apply from 2 August 2026 within their scope; this does not collapse copyright, personality or contract analysis.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留為跨域 horizontal overlay；只在本卡條件被案件觸發時疊加，不取代案件原本的 primary substantive route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-097-ARTIFICIAL_INTELLIGENCE_AI_GOVERNANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "人工智慧／AI治理法"
  english_name: "Artificial Intelligence / AI Governance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "哪個AI model / system / application在何種lifecycle stage，由哪個provider / deployer / importer / distributor / public authority使用於哪種purpose；適用法律如何分類risk / prohibited use / transparency / general-purpose / sectoral obligations；data、evaluation、human oversight、logging、security、incident、affected-person rights與remedy如何被接成可追溯治理鏈？"

  historical_reason:
    "AI把預測、分類、生成與排序能力嵌入就業、金融、教育、醫療、治安、行政與消費市場，使一個模型可跨領域反覆部署。傳統sector law仍提供實體權利，但很難單獨處理general-purpose model、model lifecycle、training / evaluation、human oversight、systemic risk與跨供應鏈責任。AI governance law因此形成horizontal risk rules，再與data、product safety、human rights、consumer、labour與行政法交叉。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "innovation vs fundamental rights / safety"
    - "general-purpose models vs downstream context"
    - "provider control vs deployer control"
    - "automation benefits vs meaningful human oversight"
    - "transparency vs trade secrets / cybersecurity"
    - "explainability vs technical complexity"
    - "statistical performance vs individual justice"
    - "benchmark results vs real-world deployment"
    - "data scale vs privacy / IP / provenance"
    - "open-source development vs accountability"
    - "fast model updates vs regulatory conformity"
    - "public-sector efficiency vs due process"
    - "risk prevention vs premature factual closure"

  shortest_maxim:
    "AI法第一眼不是『這是AI所以危險／先進』；先問『哪個system、哪個version、誰在什麼角色、為什麼目的部署、輸出實際影響誰、哪個法定分類與sector law適用、誰能理解／拒絕／修正它的決定』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 103.1 第一眼｜MODEL / SYSTEM → USE CASE → ACTOR / ROLE → LIFECYCLE → LEGAL CLASSIFICATION → INPUT / DATA → OUTPUT / DECISION ROLE → EVALUATION → HUMAN OVERSIGHT → TRANSPARENCY / LOGGING → INCIDENT → REMEDY

```text
MODEL / SYSTEM
＝foundation / GPAI model？classifier？generator？embedded system？

USE CASE
＝employment？credit？education？health？police？consumer? public administration?

ACTOR / ROLE
＝provider？deployer？importer？distributor？integrator？affected person?

LIFECYCLE
＝training？fine-tuning？evaluation？placing on market？deployment？update?

LEGAL CLASSIFICATION
＝prohibited？high-risk？transparency? general-purpose? sector-regulated?

INPUT / DATA
＝personal data？licensed data？sensor data？user prompt？

OUTPUT / DECISION ROLE
＝advice？ranking？score？automated decision？draft?

EVALUATION
＝accuracy？robustness？bias？red-team？context performance?

HUMAN OVERSIGHT
＝who can understand / intervene / override?

TRANSPARENCY / LOGGING
＝notice？technical documentation？logs？reason?

INCIDENT
＝harm？misuse？security failure？model drift?

REMEDY
＝complaint？human review？regulator？court？recall / withdrawal?
```

```yaml
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"

  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B33"
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "NIS2_TRANSPOSITION_DEADLINE_PASSED_2024_10_17_WITH_INCOMPLETE_MEMBER_STATE_IMPLEMENTATION__CRA_REPORTING_STARTS_2026_09_11_AND_MAIN_OBLIGATIONS_2027_12_11__SECTOR_INCIDENT_AND_SUPPLY_CHAIN_GATES_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

NIS2’s transposition deadline passed in 2024 but the Commission still reported incomplete implementation in several Member States in July 2026. CRA reporting duties begin 11 September 2026 and main obligations only in December 2027, so CURRENT != FULLY APPLICABLE.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — NIS2 infringement/referral update**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/news/commission-refers-ireland-spain-france-and-netherlands-court-justice-failing-transpose-rules
  - Verified point: The 17 October 2024 transposition deadline has passed, but the Commission still reported incomplete transposition in several Member States in July 2026.
- **European Commission — Cyber Resilience Act summary**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/cra-summary
  - Verified point: CRA reporting obligations begin 11 September 2026 and the main obligations apply from 11 December 2027; entry into force is not full current applicability.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留為跨域 horizontal overlay；只在本卡條件被案件觸發時疊加，不取代案件原本的 primary substantive route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-098-CYBERSECURITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "網路安全／資安法"
  english_name: "Cybersecurity Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "哪個network / information system / digital product / critical service、誰控制asset與supply chain、面臨何種threat / vulnerability / exposure；誰負risk-management、secure design、access control、vulnerability handling、incident reporting、business continuity、recovery與regulator cooperation義務；何時另轉入cybercrime、data breach、national security、product safety與electronic-evidence程序？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代社會把金融、醫療、能源、通信、政府與生活用品連入同一digital dependency graph。傳統criminal law只能處理部分攻擊者，無法要求組織在攻擊前做patching、segmentation、backup、supplier controls與incident response。資安法因此把責任前移，形成entity-level risk management、critical-infrastructure resilience、product cybersecurity、vulnerability disclosure、incident reporting與跨境CSIRT cooperation。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "security monitoring vs privacy"
    - "rapid disclosure vs investigation / confidentiality"
    - "patch speed vs operational stability"
    - "open vulnerability research vs anti-hacking law"
    - "supplier efficiency vs systemic concentration"
    - "security by design vs legacy systems"
    - "incident transparency vs reputational / national-security concerns"
    - "mandatory reporting vs self-incrimination fears"
    - "ransom response vs sanctions / public policy"
    - "cross-border evidence vs sovereignty / data safeguards"
    - "certification vs evolving threats"
    - "critical-service continuity vs shutdown containment"
    - "State cyber defence vs private network autonomy"

  shortest_maxim:
    "資安法第一眼不是『被駭了』；先問『哪個asset、哪個threat、哪個vulnerability、誰控制預防與回復、事件究竟是attempt / exploit / incident / data breach / crime哪一層、法律要求何時通報、誰能取得哪些logs / evidence』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 104.1 第一眼｜ASSET / SERVICE → ACTOR → THREAT → VULNERABILITY → EXPOSURE → SECURITY DUTY → PREVENTION → DETECTION → INCIDENT → REPORTING → CONTAINMENT / RECOVERY → FORENSICS → LIABILITY / REVIEW

```text
ASSET / SERVICE
＝network？cloud？hospital？power? software product?

ACTOR
＝operator？provider？supplier？managed service? user?

THREAT
＝criminal? state-linked? insider? accidental?

VULNERABILITY
＝software flaw? weak config? credentials? supplier?

EXPOSURE
＝internet-facing? privileged path? critical dependency?

SECURITY DUTY
＝statute? licence? contract? product requirement?

PREVENTION
＝patch? MFA? segmentation? backup? secure design?

DETECTION
＝SOC? EDR? logs? alert?

INCIDENT
＝attempt? compromise? outage? exfiltration?

REPORTING
＝CSIRT? regulator? data authority? customer?

CONTAINMENT / RECOVERY
＝isolate? restore? rotate keys? continuity?

FORENSICS
＝preserve logs / image / chain?

LIABILITY / REVIEW
＝regulator? contract? tort? criminal investigation?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 104.35 Evidence architecture

- asset inventory；
- network diagram；
- threat / risk assessment；
- vulnerability scan；
- patch records；
- IAM logs；
- EDR / SIEM；
- supplier contracts；
- backups / restore tests；
- incident timeline；
- forensic images；
- data-flow map；
- notification；
- regulator response；
- cybercrime orders；
- ransom / payment records；
- post-incident review。

核心：

> **資安案必須把「系統弱點」「攻擊者行為」「組織控制」「實際損害」拆開；否則最容易把一個技術漏洞直接寫成過失，或把一場outage直接寫成犯罪。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "BUILDING_CONSTRUCTION_LAW_JURISDICTION_SPECIFIC__BUILDING_SAFETY_CLIMATE_BIM_STANDARD_FORM_AND_CONTRACTOR_INSOLVENCY_RULES_REQUIRE_LOCAL_CURRENT_LAW_CHECK"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Construction law remains intensely jurisdiction-specific. Building safety, permitting, contractor licensing, standard forms, insolvency, BIM and climate duties must be checked against the project location and contract; no transnational status can close the card.

### CURRENT-LAW GATE RESULT

- No single transnational source was allowed to masquerade as the governing domestic law. Runtime must resolve jurisdiction, date, object/system/resource classification and local positive law before merits closure.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-020-CONSTRUCTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "建築工程法"
  english_name: "Construction Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當工程是一個長期、分階段、多專業、持續變更、成果在現場逐步形成且錯誤可能相互放大的生產過程時，法律如何分配設計、施工、工期、價金、風險、變更、瑕疵、驗收與索賠責任？"

  historical_reason:
    "工程不是一次性交付成品。業主、設計人、監造、總包、分包、材料商與政府機關可能在數年內反覆提供圖說、指示、進度、付款與變更。延誤與瑕疵往往有多重原因，且錯誤一旦被後續工序覆蓋就難以回復。工程法因此高度重視契約風險分配、變更程序、通知、紀錄、工期分析、驗收與爭端管理。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "固定價格 vs 不可預見變更"
    - "業主需求變更 vs 承包商工期／價金調整"
    - "設計責任 vs 施工責任"
    - "進度效率 vs 品質／安全"
    - "總包統籌 vs 分包專業責任"
    - "現場實況 vs 契約圖說"
    - "及時通知 vs 實質權利"
    - "完工交付 vs 瑕疵修補"
    - "工程不中斷 vs 爭議即時解決"

  shortest_maxim:
    "工程法第一眼不是『誰做錯』；先問『誰負責設計、誰下指示、誰承擔這個風險、什麼時候發生、是否有通知、變更、延誤與紀錄』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 26.1 第一眼｜PROJECT → CONTRACT MODEL → ROLE → RISK → EVENT → NOTICE → RECORD

```text
PROJECT
＝住宅？廠房？公共工程？EPC？基礎建設？維修？

CONTRACT MODEL
＝傳統設計—招標—施工？Design-Build？EPC？管理承攬？PPP？

ROLE
＝業主？建築師？工程師？監造？總包？分包？供應商？

RISK ALLOCATION
＝地質、設計、材料、價格、法令變更、天候、許可誰負擔？

EVENT
＝變更？延誤？停工？瑕疵？事故？追加？

NOTICE
＝契約是否要求限期通知？

RECORD
＝圖說、RFI、site instruction、日誌、進度表、付款、照片是否完整？
```

Urban planning and land-use law is primarily local. Zoning, development permission, contributions, housing policy, climate adaptation and judicial review remain jurisdiction- and parcel-specific.

### CURRENT-LAW GATE RESULT

- No single transnational source was allowed to masquerade as the governing domestic law. Runtime must resolve jurisdiction, date, object/system/resource classification and local positive law before merits closure.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 BUILT_ENVIRONMENT_LAND_DEVELOPMENT 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-088-URBAN_PLANNING_LAND_USE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "都市計畫／土地使用法"
  english_name: "Urban Planning / Land Use Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家／地方政府如何透過 national / regional / metropolitan / municipal / detailed plans 配置 housing、commerce、industry、transport、public space、environment與infrastructure；土地 owner / developer 在何種 zoning、density、subdivision、permit、impact、infrastructure contribution、renewal / expropriation、participation 與 review 條件下取得 development qualification？"

  historical_reason:
    "都市土地彼此高度相依：一塊地的高度、道路出入口、污染、人口密度與用途會改變鄰地、交通、學校、排水與公共設施負荷。單靠 property law 無法處理 cumulative spatial effects，因此 modern planning law 以 plan hierarchy、zoning、development control、public infrastructure、land assembly、urban renewal、participation、value capture、environmental integration 與 judicial review，把私人 parcel 納入共同城市結構。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "private property vs collective spatial order"
    - "planning discretion vs legal certainty"
    - "long-term plan vs changing social needs"
    - "density / growth vs infrastructure capacity"
    - "housing supply vs displacement"
    - "heritage conservation vs redevelopment"
    - "economic development vs environment / resilience"
    - "public participation vs decision efficiency"
    - "land-value increase vs private windfall / public capture"
    - "informal settlements vs formal planning"
    - "metropolitan reality vs municipal boundaries"
    - "transit investment vs property impacts"
    - "expropriation / compulsory assembly vs owner rights"

  shortest_maxim:
    "都市計畫法第一眼不是『這塊地是我的』；先問『它位在哪一層 plan、目前 zoning / density / infrastructure capacity 是什麼、要改變哪一種 land use、需要哪個 planning / building / environmental gate、誰會因這次變更受益或承擔負擔』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 94.1 第一眼｜TERRITORY / PARCEL → PLAN HIERARCHY → ZONING / DESIGNATION → EXISTING USE → DEVELOPMENT PROPOSAL → INFRASTRUCTURE → IMPACT / PUBLIC INTEREST → PARTICIPATION → PERMIT → VALUE / COMPENSATION → REVIEW

```text
TERRITORY / PARCEL
＝national / regional / metropolitan / municipal / site？

PLAN HIERARCHY
＝strategic plan？master plan？regulatory plan？detailed plan？

ZONING / DESIGNATION
＝residential？commercial？industrial？mixed？conservation？

EXISTING USE
＝lawful existing use？non-conforming use？informal use？

DEVELOPMENT PROPOSAL
＝subdivision？change of use？height / FAR？renewal？infrastructure？

INFRASTRUCTURE
＝road？water？sewer？school？transit？power？

IMPACT / PUBLIC INTEREST
＝traffic？housing？heritage？environment？climate？safety？

PARTICIPATION
＝notice？hearing？consultation？referendum where any？

PERMIT
＝planning approval？development permit？building permit？

VALUE / COMPENSATION
＝exaction？betterment？land readjustment？expropriation？

REVIEW
＝administrative appeal？judicial review？constitutional claim？
```

- cadastral / title；

> **都市計畫案不能只看地圖顏色；要追這個顏色是哪一層 plan、何時生效、對哪一種行為有拘束力，以及從圖上願景到個別 parcel 的權利變化中間還有哪些法定 gates。**
