EU environmental law is multi-layered. The Nature Restoration Regulation is in force and draft national restoration plans are due 1 September 2026, but permits, liability, pollution controls and national implementation remain separate.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Nature Restoration Regulation**
  - URL: https://environment.ec.europa.eu/topics/nature-and-biodiversity/nature-restoration-regulation_en
  - Verified point: The Regulation is in force; draft national restoration plans are due by 1 September 2026.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-11／INT-12 的 PARTIAL_SUPERSESSION_STRUCTURAL 決定，環境法保留共同概念、入口與 router 法位；具體專門卡在自身 first move 取得優先，來源母卡不得被刪除，也不得重新擴張成專門卡的第二份 authoritative body。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-022-ENVIRONMENTAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "環境法"
  english_name: "Environmental Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當污染、開發與資源利用的利益由特定人取得，但成本可能被轉嫁給鄰居、社區、生態系、下游地區與未來世代時，法律如何在損害發生前辨識風險、在活動進行中監測控制、在損害發生後修復並配置責任？"

  historical_reason:
    "環境損害常具有累積性、不可逆性、跨界性與科學不確定性。若只依一般侵權法等到具體受害者證明因果與損害後才介入，許多污染、生態破壞與資源枯竭可能已無法回復。因此環境法發展出環評、許可、排放標準、最佳可行技術、監測、資訊公開、預防與污染者負擔等前置治理制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "經濟開發 vs 環境品質"
    - "個別財產利用 vs 生態系共同利益"
    - "科學不確定性 vs 及時預防"
    - "污染防制成本 vs 外部成本內部化"
    - "中央政策 vs 地方與社區承受"
    - "專業行政判斷 vs 公眾參與與司法審查"
    - "現代利益 vs 未來世代"
    - "污染者負擔 vs 歷史污染／多重污染源"
    - "環境資訊透明 vs 商業秘密"

  shortest_maxim:
    "環境法第一眼不是『有人受傷了嗎』；先問『哪個活動造成哪一種環境風險、風險會往哪裡走、誰能事前控制、誰負監測與修復責任』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 28.1 第一眼｜ACTIVITY → MEDIUM → PATHWAY → RECEPTOR → PERMIT → PREVENTION → REMEDY

```text
ACTIVITY
＝工廠？開發？採礦？農業？廢棄物？交通？能源？建築？

MEDIUM
＝空氣？水？土壤？地下水？海洋？噪音？生態？

PATHWAY
＝污染物／風險如何移動？

RECEPTOR
＝居民？勞工？河川？農地？保育物種？下游社區？

PERMIT / STANDARD
＝需要什麼許可？排放標準？總量？設施要求？

PREVENTION
＝環評？最佳可行技術？監測？應變？

REMEDY
＝停止？改善？清除？復育？賠償？行政／刑事責任？
```

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-024-CLIMATE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "氣候法"
  english_name: "Climate Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當溫室氣體排放來自全球大量分散行為、影響跨數十年至數世代，且單一排放與單一災害之間難以用傳統一對一因果模型處理時，法律如何把氣候科學轉化成減量、調適、國家承諾、企業轉型、資訊揭露與損失分配？"

  historical_reason:
    "氣候變遷不是典型地方污染：溫室氣體在全球累積，排放與影響跨境，受害與排放在時間與空間上分離。傳統環境許可只能處理部分排放源，單一侵權訴訟也難以獨自解決全球累積問題。因此氣候法同時從國際條約、國內目標、碳定價、能源轉型、企業揭露、調適、氣候訴訟與金融監管等多層形成。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "全球共同問題 vs 國家主權"
    - "歷史排放 vs 當代與未來責任"
    - "減量速度 vs 經濟與社會轉型成本"
    - "科學風險 vs 法律因果"
    - "全球目標 vs 國內執行"
    - "企業承諾 vs 可驗證轉型"
    - "減量 vs 調適"
    - "氣候金融 vs greenwashing"
    - "當代利益 vs 未來世代"
    - "公平分擔 vs 能力與發展差異"

  shortest_maxim:
    "氣候法第一眼不是『這場颱風是不是某家公司造成』；先問『哪一層法律義務、哪一組排放、哪一個時間尺度、哪一種減量／調適責任、如何把科學風險轉成可執行法律資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 30.1 第一眼｜EMISSIONS → ACTOR → LEGAL LEVEL → TARGET → INSTRUMENT → EVIDENCE → ACCOUNTABILITY

```text
EMISSIONS
＝哪種溫室氣體？scope / sector / source？

ACTOR
＝國家？地方政府？電廠？石油公司？金融機構？一般企業？

＝UNFCCC／Paris？國內氣候法？能源法？證券揭露？侵權？行政法？

TARGET / DUTY
＝NDC？法定減量目標？許可上限？揭露？transition plan？

INSTRUMENT
＝碳稅？ETS？標準？補貼？禁售？能效？土地利用？

EVIDENCE
＝排放清冊？情境模型？attribution science？企業資料？

ACCOUNTABILITY
＝誰監督？法院能審什麼？未達目標的法律效果？
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 ENVIRONMENT_CLIMATE_ASSESSMENT 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-199-ENVIRONMENTAL_IMPACT_ASSESSMENT_ENVIRONMENTAL_PERMITTING_PROCEDURE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "環境影響評估／環境許可程序法"
  english_name: "Environmental Impact Assessment / Environmental Permitting Procedure Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "開發project、plan/programme或重大活動在取得環境許可／development consent以前，何時必須screening、scoping、建立baseline、評估direct/indirect/cumulative effects、提出合理替代方案與mitigation，讓public、affected State與特定indigenous/community依法參與；主管機關最後如何把評估真正帶入decision、conditions、monitoring與後續變更，而不是把EIA當成一份附檔報告？"

  historical_reason:
    "環境損害往往在工程建成、資本投入與土地改變後才完全顯現，而那時候再以侵權賠償處理往往太晚。EIA因此把『資訊不足』本身提前變成決策程序問題；SEA則把相同邏輯提早到plan/programme層。Espoo/Aarhus與EU制度又使跨境通知、公眾參與、替代方案、理由與司法救濟形成自己的程序世界。"

  first_move:
    "先固定 proposed action與真正的decision level：project / plan / programme / policy，再判斷screening與applicable EIA/SEA regime；之後才進scoping、baseline、alternatives、impact, mitigation、participation、transboundary/indigenous interfaces、reasoned decision、conditions、monitoring與project change。不得以『主管機關已核准環評』直接證明project無害或所有其他permit已取得。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "development speed vs information quality"
    - "project certainty vs alternatives"
    - "technical expertise vs public participation"
    - "national decision vs transboundary effects"
    - "project-level EIA vs strategic SEA"
    - "developer-funded studies vs assessment independence"
    - "prediction uncertainty vs precaution"
    - "mitigation promises vs enforceable conditions"
    - "approval vs monitoring / adaptive management"
    - "indigenous consultation vs general public participation"
    - "fragmented permits vs cumulative effects"
    - "procedural compliance vs substantive environmental outcome"

  shortest_maxim:
    "環評第一眼不是『報告有沒有做』；先問『這個決策在什麼時點必須知道哪些環境後果、比較哪些替代方案、讓誰參與、由誰判斷、結果怎麼真正寫進許可與後續監測』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-60.1 第一眼｜ACTION → LEVEL → SCREENING → SCOPING → BASELINE → ALTERNATIVES → IMPACT → MITIGATION → PARTICIPATION → TRANSBOUNDARY / INDIGENOUS → DECISION → CONDITIONS → MONITORING → CHANGE / REVIEW

```text
ACTION
＝project? expansion? plan? programme? policy?

LEVEL
＝project EIA or strategic assessment?

SCREENING
＝mandatory list? threshold? case-by-case?

SCOPING
＝which issues / geography / time horizon?

BASELINE
＝what is current environment without project?

ALTERNATIVES
＝site? design? technology? zero / no-project?

IMPACT
＝direct? indirect? cumulative? climate? biodiversity? health?

MITIGATION
＝avoid? minimize? restore? compensate?

PARTICIPATION
＝public? affected community? expert bodies?

TRANSBOUNDARY / INDIGENOUS
＝affected State? indigenous consultation / FPIC interface?

DECISION
＝how did authority use assessment?

CONDITIONS
＝permit conditions / mitigation commitments?

MONITORING
＝post-approval compliance / effects?

CHANGE / REVIEW
＝modified project? new information? supplemental assessment?
```

- affected-State comments；
- EIA report；
- independent review；
- development consent；
- permit conditions；
- monitoring plan；
- compliance data；
- modification application；

The CAP 2023–27 framework is current, while EUDR application is still future-phased to 30 December 2026 / 30 June 2027 by operator size. Agricultural subsidies, plant/animal health, seed rules and national implementation remain dynamic.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — CAP and environment**
  - URL: https://agriculture.ec.europa.eu/cap-my-country/sustainability/environmental-sustainability/cap-and-environment_en
  - Verified point: The CAP 2023–27 framework is current; concrete subsidy, conditionality and national strategic-plan rules remain scheme and jurisdiction specific.
- **European Commission — Regulation on deforestation-free products**
  - URL: https://environment.ec.europa.eu/topics/forests/deforestation/regulation-deforestation-free-products_en
  - Verified point: EUDR application is deferred to 30 December 2026 for large/medium operators and 30 June 2027 for micro/small operators, subject to the official scope rules.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-079-AGRICULTURAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "農業法"
  english_name: "Agricultural Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "誰有權使用土地、水、種子、動植物與農業基礎設施生產糧食／農產品；農業經營者、土地所有人、承租人、contract grower、agricultural worker、cooperative、processor、buyer 與 State 的關係如何被 land、labour、plant / animal health、seed、subsidy、trade、food safety、environment 與 genetic-resources law 共同包住？"

  historical_reason:
    "農業不是一般製造業：生產依賴土地、水、氣候、生物、season 與長週期投資，同時承擔 food security、rural livelihood、environmental stewardship 與價格風險。國家因此長期以土地制度、農業信貸、補貼、保價、保險、檢疫與 trade measures 介入；現代農業法必須把 production、tenure、labour、biosecurity、genetic resources、market power、food safety 與 WTO disciplines 接成同一生命週期。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "private land rights vs food-security / land-use policy"
    - "farmer autonomy vs contract / buyer power"
    - "production support vs trade distortion"
    - "seed innovation vs farmers' practices / genetic diversity"
    - "plant health vs trade facilitation"
    - "biosecurity vs scientific uncertainty"
    - "intensive production vs soil / water / biodiversity"
    - "family farming vs industrial agriculture"
    - "agricultural labour flexibility vs worker safety"
    - "price stabilization vs market competition"
    - "food security vs export / import restrictions"
    - "farm data / precision agriculture vs control / privacy"
    - "climate adaptation vs legacy land / water entitlements"

  shortest_maxim:
    "農業法第一眼不是『這是一塊農地』；先問『誰在什麼土地／水／生產資格下種什麼、用什麼種苗與投入品、由誰工作、賣給誰、受哪個補貼／檢疫／食品／貿易制度影響』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 85.1 第一眼｜LAND / TENURE → PRODUCER STATUS → WATER / INPUT → CROP / LIVESTOCK → LABOUR → BIOSECURITY → CONTRACT / MARKET → SUPPORT → FOOD / TRADE → ENVIRONMENT

```text
＝owner？tenant？customary holder？cooperative？

＝family farmer？company？contract grower？livestock operator？

FOOD / TRADE

ENVIRONMENT
＝soil、water、biodiversity、emissions、waste？
```

## 85.29 證據特徵

- land title / lease；

> **農業案件不能用一張「農民證明」或「農地謄本」取代完整生產鏈；真正需要重建的是誰控制土地、投入、生物、勞動、價格與風險。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "FISHERIES_LAW_HIGHLY_DYNAMIC__EU_2026_TAC_QUOTAS_CURRENT_AND_STOCK_SPECIFIC__CONTROL_RULES_HAVE_2026_AND_2028_APPLICATION_PHASES__RFMO_IUU_SEASON_GEAR_AND_CATCH_DOCUMENTATION_REQUIRE_DATE_AND_STOCK_CHECK"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Fisheries law is inherently time-, stock- and area-specific. EU 2026 TAC/quotas are current and amended by stock, while revised control rules have staged application in 2026 and 2028; RFMO, IUU, gear and season rules must be rechecked for the fishery.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — TACs and quotas 2026**
  - URL: https://oceans-and-fisheries.ec.europa.eu/fisheries-management/fishing-quotas/tacs-and-quotas-2026_en
  - Verified point: 2026 fishing opportunities are fixed by stock/area and include regulations and amendments for 2026, 2027 and 2028; they are inherently date- and stock-specific.
- **European Commission — Fisheries Control Regulation**
  - URL: https://oceans-and-fisheries.ec.europa.eu/fisheries-management/enforcing-rules/control-regulation_en
  - Verified point: Some revised control rules apply in 2026 while others apply only in 2028; CATCH import certification became mandatory from 10 January 2026.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-080-FISHERIES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "漁業法"
  english_name: "Fisheries Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "誰依何種海域／水域 jurisdiction 有權捕撈哪種 living aquatic resources；vessel、flag、licence、quota、gear、season、species、bycatch、reporting、landing、port inspection、catch documentation 與 market traceability 如何形成合法捕撈鏈；coastal State、flag State、port State、RFMO 與 market State 如何共同管理共享／洄游資源並打擊 IUU fishing？"

  historical_reason:
    "魚類會移動、跨邊界、難以在捕撈前個別所有，且過度捕撈會把所有人的未來資源一起消耗。漁業法因此不是普通 property law，而是 conservation science、maritime jurisdiction、vessel control、allocation、community tenure、labour、subsidies、port controls、catch documentation 與 international cooperation 的混合法。IUU fishing 更證明單靠 flag State 不足，需要 coastal、port、market 與 RFMO 多層 governance。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "harvest rights vs conservation"
    - "coastal State competence vs high-seas freedoms"
    - "industrial fleet vs small-scale / customary fisheries"
    - "flag State responsibility vs convenience registration"
    - "quota efficiency vs equitable allocation"
    - "science uncertainty vs precaution"
    - "target species vs bycatch / ecosystem impacts"
    - "port access vs IUU enforcement"
    - "traceability vs fisher compliance burden"
    - "crew labour rights vs distant-water operations"
    - "fisheries subsidies vs overcapacity"
    - "food security vs export markets"
    - "RFMO collective management vs State sovereignty"

  shortest_maxim:
    "漁業法第一眼不是『魚在哪裡抓到』；先問『哪個海域／stock、哪艘船、哪個 flag、哪張 licence / authorization、哪個 quota / gear / season、怎麼申報與卸魚、哪個 port / market rule 接手』。"
```

The EU Critical Raw Materials Act is current and strategic-project status is dynamic. Mining permits, royalties, closure, tailings, indigenous rights and environmental approvals remain domestic/project-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Critical Raw Materials Act strategic projects**
  - URL: https://single-market-economy.ec.europa.eu/calls-expression-interest/call-applications-strategic-projects-under-critical-raw-materials-act_en
  - Verified point: CRMA is current and strategic-project lists/calls are dynamic; project status does not substitute for domestic mining permits and closure duties.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-081-MINING_MINERAL_RESOURCES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "礦業法"
  english_name: "Mining / Mineral Resources Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "地下／地表礦產在一個法域中由誰擁有或由國家如何保留；exploration、mineral title、mining licence / concession、surface access、environmental and water permits、indigenous/community rights、royalty / tax、mine safety、tailings、closure、rehabilitation 與 post-closure responsibility 如何形成完整 extraction lifecycle？"

  historical_reason:
    "礦產不可再生、位置固定、資本密集，且 mine life 可跨越數十年。採礦能產生公共收入與關鍵材料，也會造成 land disturbance、water risk、tailings、worker hazards、community displacement 與 closure liabilities。礦業法因此必須把 mineral ownership、licensing、fiscal terms、environmental / social review、labour safety、transparency、beneficial ownership、waste / tailings、closure financial assurance 與 rehabilitation 從第一張 exploration permit 一路接到最後 post-closure monitoring。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "State mineral sovereignty vs private investment security"
    - "mineral title vs surface / community rights"
    - "development revenue vs environmental / social harm"
    - "licence certainty vs changing regulation"
    - "national benefit vs local community burden"
    - "indigenous consent / participation vs project approval"
    - "technical discretion vs transparent allocation"
    - "production speed vs mine safety"
    - "tailings storage vs catastrophic risk"
    - "current revenue vs closure liabilities"
    - "resource nationalism vs investment / trade rules"
    - "critical-mineral transition demand vs biodiversity"
    - "artisanal / small-scale mining vs formal licensing"

  shortest_maxim:
    "礦業法第一眼不是『地下有礦』；先問『誰依法擁有／管理 mineral、哪個 title / licence 在哪個區塊、做到 exploration 還是 extraction、surface / water / environment / community gates 是否另行取得、closure 誰付錢』。"
```

Water law depends on jurisdiction, basin and treaty. Allocation, abstraction, groundwater, ecological flow, drought/flood control, transboundary obligations and human-rights access therefore remain a live local/treaty gate.

### CURRENT-LAW GATE RESULT

- No single transnational source was allowed to masquerade as the governing domestic law. Runtime must resolve jurisdiction, date, object/system/resource classification and local positive law before merits closure.

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-082-WATER_RESOURCES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "水資源法"
  english_name: "Water Resources Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "河川、湖泊、水庫、地下水、濕地與流域中的 water quantity / quality 由誰管理；個人、農業、工業、城市、生態系與跨境鄰國如何取得 abstraction / allocation / service / discharge 位置；human right to water、drought、flood、dam、ecological flow、transboundary cooperation 與 groundwater governance 如何在一條可追溯水循環中共存？"

  historical_reason:
    "水會流動、滲透、蒸發、再補注，且同一水體往往同時供應飲用、農業、工業、能源、航運、生態與文化需求。土地法無法單獨決定水的分配；環境法也無法單獨處理 priority、allocation 與 infrastructure。水資源法因此將 hydrology、allocation、human rights、public service、pollution control、basin planning、drought/flood、wetlands 與 transboundary cooperation 接成一個動態治理系統。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "private / licensed use vs public-resource character"
    - "human right to water vs productive allocation"
    - "prior / vested rights vs adaptive drought management"
    - "surface-water rules vs groundwater reality"
    - "upstream use vs downstream harm"
    - "quantity allocation vs water quality"
    - "infrastructure reliability vs ecological flow"
    - "tariff recovery vs affordability"
    - "flood-control infrastructure vs displacement / ecosystems"
    - "national sovereignty vs transboundary cooperation"
    - "historic irrigation rights vs climate scarcity"
    - "wetland conservation vs drainage / development"
    - "data uncertainty vs allocation certainty"

  shortest_maxim:
    "水資源法第一眼不是『水在誰土地上』；先問『是哪一個 water body / aquifer、誰依法分配、誰持何種 abstraction / service / discharge 權限、可用水量多少、哪個時段、對下游與生態有何影響、乾旱時誰先縮減』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 88.1 第一眼｜WATER BODY / AQUIFER → JURISDICTION → RIGHT / PERMIT → QUANTITY / PRIORITY → USE → QUALITY / DISCHARGE → ECOLOGY → INFRASTRUCTURE → DROUGHT / FLOOD → TRANSBOUNDARY → REMEDY

```text

＝river？lake？reservoir？wetland？groundwater？transboundary system？

JURISDICTION
＝national agency？basin authority？local utility？international commission？

RIGHT / PERMIT
＝riparian？appropriative？licence？concession？customary right？service entitlement？

QUANTITY / PRIORITY
＝volume？rate？season？priority date？minimum flow？

USE
＝drinking？agriculture？industry？energy？ecosystem？cultural？

QUALITY / DISCHARGE
＝abstraction standard？effluent permit？pollution source？

＝environmental flow？wetland？aquatic habitat？

＝dam？canal？well？treatment plant？utility？

＝curtailment？emergency order？reservoir rule？

TRANSBOUNDARY
＝equitable use？harm？notification？joint body？

REMEDY
＝appeal？compensation？injunction？treaty consultation？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 88.32 證據特徵

- basin map；
- water-right / permit register；
- priority / allocation records；
- well logs；
- pumping meter；
- stream gauges；
- reservoir data；

- tariffs；

核心：

> **水案不能只問「誰有紙本權利」，還要問「水到底有沒有、從哪裡來、往哪裡去、抽掉後誰少了、排回去後變成什麼」。**

On 25 August 2026, EUDR application is still future-phased to 30 December 2026 for large/medium operators and 30 June 2027 for micro/small operators. BUILD02 therefore forbids APPLICATION BEFORE APPLICATION DATE; tenure, fire, carbon and forestry licensing remain local.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Regulation on deforestation-free products**
  - URL: https://environment.ec.europa.eu/topics/forests/deforestation/regulation-deforestation-free-products_en
  - Verified point: EUDR application is deferred to 30 December 2026 for large/medium operators and 30 June 2027 for micro/small operators, subject to the official scope rules.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 NATURAL_RESOURCE_BIOSPHERE_USE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-083-FOREST_LAW"
  chinese_name: "森林法"
  english_name: "Forest Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "何種 land / vegetation 在法律上屬 forest、誰擁有土地與 forest resources、誰取得 concession / harvest / community-use / carbon / conservation position；從 forest planning、tree felling、transport、processing、trade、fire、restoration 到 carbon / biodiversity governance，哪些權利與義務必須一路追蹤？"

  historical_reason:
    "森林同時是土地、木材來源、水源涵養、碳庫、野生動物棲地、文化空間與社群生計。只用 land title 會忽略 forest resources 與 community tenure；只用 environmental law 又無法回答誰能採伐、如何分配 concession、誰收 royalty、木材如何證明合法來源。森林法因此形成 tenure、classification、management planning、harvest authorization、chain of custody、fire、restoration、community / indigenous rights、forest carbon 與 anti-illegal-logging 的交叉制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "forest tenure vs State control"
    - "timber production vs biodiversity / watershed"
    - "concession investment vs community / customary rights"
    - "harvest flexibility vs long rotation cycles"
    - "forest carbon vs land / resource ownership"
    - "fire suppression vs ecological fire regimes"
    - "plantation productivity vs natural-forest biodiversity"
    - "restoration targets vs local livelihood"
    - "certification vs public regulation"
    - "illegal logging enforcement vs due process"
    - "national revenue vs local benefit sharing"
    - "forest conversion vs agriculture / mining expansion"
    - "indigenous knowledge vs centralized forestry"

  shortest_maxim:
    "森林法第一眼不是『這裡有樹』；先問『法律如何分類這塊 land、誰有 forest / timber / customary / concession rights、這次行為是 harvest、conversion、fire、restoration 還是 carbon project、每一批 timber / credit 如何追到來源』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 89.1 第一眼｜FOREST / LAND CLASS → TENURE → RESOURCE RIGHT → MANAGEMENT PLAN → HARVEST / CONVERSION → TRANSPORT → PROCESSING / TRADE → FIRE / BIODIVERSITY → CARBON → RESTORATION → REVIEW

```text
FOREST / LAND CLASS
＝natural forest？plantation？protected forest？production forest？community forest？

TENURE
＝State？private？community？indigenous？customary？

RESOURCE RIGHT
＝ownership？concession？lease？harvest permit？NTFP rights？carbon?

MANAGEMENT PLAN
＝rotation？annual allowable cut？protected zones？

HARVEST / CONVERSION
＝logging？clearing？road？agriculture conversion？

TRANSPORT
＝log permit？marking？waybill？

PROCESSING / TRADE
＝mill？export？chain of custody？

FIRE / BIODIVERSITY
＝burn permit？habitat / species rules？

CARBON
＝REDD+ / project / credit / permanence？

RESTORATION
＝reforestation？natural regeneration？closure?

REVIEW
＝forest agency、court、community mechanism？
```

Wildlife/nature rules are dynamic by species, habitat and date. CITES listings change and the EU Nature Restoration national-plan deadline is 1 September 2026; domestic species/habitat protection and ABS rules remain separate.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **CITES — official species checklist/index**
  - URL: https://cites.org/sites/default/files/eng/resources/pub/checklist11/CITES_species_index.pdf
  - Verified point: CITES listings are maintained and changeable; species/listing status must be checked at the relevant date.
- **European Commission — Nature Restoration Regulation**
  - URL: https://environment.ec.europa.eu/topics/nature-and-biodiversity/nature-restoration-regulation_en
  - Verified point: The Regulation is in force; draft national restoration plans are due by 1 September 2026.

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-084-WILDLIFE_NATURE_CONSERVATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "野生動物／自然保育法"
  english_name: "Wildlife / Nature Conservation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "ecosystem、habitat、species、individual specimen、genetic resource 與 migratory population 如何被不同保育制度保護；誰可以 hunt / take / capture / possess / breed / transport / trade / release；protected area、species listing、CITES、CMS、CBD / GBF、ABS、invasive species、human-wildlife conflict 與 restoration 如何分層？"

  historical_reason:
    "野生物不服從地籍邊界：動物會遷徙、植物會散播，物種生存依賴 habitat、connectivity 與 population-level dynamics。只管 hunting 會漏掉 habitat loss；只設 protected area 又會漏掉 trade、invasive species 與 migratory routes。自然保育法因此從單一 specimen 擴張至 species、ecosystem、genetic diversity、protected areas、trade、migration、restoration 與 community rights。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "conservation vs sustainable use"
    - "protected species vs livelihood / customary use"
    - "protected areas vs land / indigenous rights"
    - "wildlife trade vs conservation / local economies"
    - "human-wildlife conflict vs species protection"
    - "species-level rules vs ecosystem-level needs"
    - "scientific uncertainty vs irreversible loss"
    - "ex situ conservation vs wild-population integrity"
    - "captive breeding vs laundering of wild specimens"
    - "invasive-species control vs animal welfare"
    - "genetic-resource access vs sovereign / community rights"
    - "restoration intervention vs ecological uncertainty"
    - "international listing vs domestic enforcement"

  shortest_maxim:
    "野生動物／自然保育法第一眼不是『這動物很稀有』；先問『哪個 species / population / specimen、在哪個 habitat、受哪個 listing / area / trade regime、發生的是 take、possession、trade、breeding、release 還是 habitat alteration、誰有 permit / exemption』。"
```

## 90.1 第一眼｜ECOSYSTEM / AREA → SPECIES / POPULATION → SPECIMEN → LEGAL STATUS → ACT → PERMIT / EXEMPTION → ORIGIN → TRADE / MOVEMENT → HABITAT → REMEDY

```yaml
    "能源既是可交易商品，也是維持社會運作的基礎設施與國家安全資源；法律如何在開採、發電、輸送、配電、零售、接網、定價、可靠度、安全、競爭與轉型之間配置權力與風險？"

  historical_reason:
    "電力、天然氣與燃料具有巨額固定投資、網路基礎設施、供需即時平衡、區域壟斷與國家安全特性。一般買賣法難以處理誰可以建電網、誰能接網、停電風險、備轉容量、管線安全與壟斷定價；能源法因此結合特許、公共事業監管、競爭法、環境法與資源法形成高度制度化領域。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "能源可負擔性 vs 投資回收"
    - "可靠度 vs 市場競爭"
    - "自然壟斷網路 vs 開放接取"
    - "能源安全 vs 國際依賴"
    - "化石燃料既有資產 vs 低碳轉型"
    - "再生能源快速接入 vs 電網穩定"
    - "中央能源政策 vs 地方環境與土地承擔"
    - "消費者保護 vs 成本反映"
    - "能源開發 vs 原住民族／社區與環境權益"

  shortest_maxim:
    "能源法第一眼不是『一度電多少錢』；先問『能源從哪裡來、經過哪個網路、誰控制關鍵基礎設施、誰負可靠度、誰能接入、成本最後由誰承擔』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 29.1 第一眼｜SOURCE → CONVERSION → NETWORK → MARKET → CUSTOMER → RELIABILITY → REGULATOR

```text
SOURCE
＝煤？油？天然氣？核能？水力？太陽能？風能？儲能？氫？

CONVERSION
＝採掘？煉製？發電？儲存？

NETWORK
＝輸電？配電？天然氣管線？終端？電池？

MARKET
＝垂直整合？批發市場？零售競爭？單一買方？

CUSTOMER
＝家庭？工業？關鍵設施？

RELIABILITY
＝誰平衡供需？誰維持備援與頻率？

REGULATOR
＝能源主管機關？公共事業委員會？市場營運者？
```

16. cyber / critical infrastructure；
17. 跨境能源交易；

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "PUBLIC_UTILITIES_BASIC_SERVICES_PRIMARILY_JURISDICTION_SPECIFIC__ENERGY_TELECOM_WATER_AND_OTHER_SECTOR_OVERLAYS_CURRENT__AFFORDABILITY_UNIVERSAL_SERVICE_PPP_OUTAGE_AND_SUBSIDY_RULES_LOCAL"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

Public-utility/basic-service law is primarily domestic and sector-specific. Energy, telecom, water and other overlays may supply current rules, but affordability, universal service, PPP, outage, tariff and subsidy questions remain local.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Hydrogen and decarbonised gas market**
  - URL: https://energy.ec.europa.eu/topics/markets-and-consumers/hydrogen-and-decarbonised-gas-market_en
  - Verified point: The 2024 gas/hydrogen reform entered into force and Member States had a 5 August 2026 transposition deadline; deadline passage does not prove uniform domestic implementation.
- **European Commission — Digital Networks Act**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/digital-networks-act
  - Verified point: The Commission adopted the Digital Networks Act proposal in January 2026; proposal status is not an operative replacement of the EECC.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留為跨域 horizontal overlay；只在本卡條件被案件觸發時疊加，不取代案件原本的 primary substantive route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-090-PUBLIC_UTILITIES_BASIC_SERVICES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公共事業／基礎服務法"
  english_name: "Public Utilities / Basic Services Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項具network / essential / universal-service特性的服務由誰負最終公共責任、誰投資與營運、是否為monopoly / concession / PPP / municipal enterprise、如何設定tariff、誰有access、服務品質與reliability標準是什麼、如何處理subsidy、non-payment、outage、emergency、regulatory change、termination與asset handback？"

  historical_reason:
    "水、能源、垃圾、公共運輸等基礎服務往往需要巨額 sunk investment、網路協調與長期資產，形成 natural-monopoly / concession / public-enterprise structures。若完全依市場，偏遠／低收入 users 可能無法取得服務；若完全由政府自營，又可能缺乏效率與透明問責。公共事業法因此用 sector regulator、tariff regulation、service obligation、concession / PPP、procurement、consumer protection、subsidy、investment planning、reliability、termination與public-asset governance維持『不論 ownership model 都有人對公共服務結果負責』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "cost recovery vs affordability"
    - "private investment vs public-service control"
    - "natural monopoly vs competition"
    - "tariff stability vs changing input costs"
    - "universal service vs financial sustainability"
    - "operator independence vs regulatory accountability"
    - "long-term concession certainty vs public-interest adaptation"
    - "service continuity vs right to terminate / disconnect"
    - "public ownership vs political interference"
    - "PPP innovation vs fiscal opacity"
    - "performance incentives vs cherry-picking profitable users"
    - "asset maintenance vs short-term tariff pressure"
    - "emergency intervention vs contract certainty"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公共事業法第一眼不是『這是公營還是民營』；先問『哪項 essential / network service、誰依法負 service obligation、誰擁有 asset、誰營運、誰定價、誰承擔投資／需求／事故風險、服務中斷或 contract 終止後誰接手』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 96.1 第一眼｜SERVICE → PUBLIC RESPONSIBILITY → ASSET → OPERATOR → LEGAL MODEL → REGULATOR → ACCESS → TARIFF → QUALITY / RELIABILITY → SUBSIDY → USER PROTECTION → FAILURE / TERMINATION → HANDBACK

```text
SERVICE
＝water？energy？waste？transport？district heat？other basic service？

PUBLIC RESPONSIBILITY
＝national？local？shared？

ASSET
＝public？private？mixed？concession asset？

OPERATOR
＝municipal enterprise？SOE？private concessionaire？PPP company？

LEGAL MODEL
＝licence？franchise？concession？management contract？PPP？

REGULATOR
＝ministry？municipality？independent regulator？

ACCESS
＝service area？universal obligation？connection right？

TARIFF
＝cost-based？price cap？subsidized？cross-subsidy？

QUALITY / RELIABILITY
＝continuity？pressure？frequency？response time？

SUBSIDY
＝budget？tariff support？lifeline? viability gap?

USER PROTECTION
＝billing？metering？complaint？disconnection？

FAILURE / TERMINATION
＝default？insolvency？emergency？step-in？

HANDBACK
＝asset condition？compensation？continuity?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 96.40 證據特徵

- enabling statute；
- service licence；
- concession / franchise；
- PPP contract；
- procurement record；
- asset register；
- tariff order / model；
- cost-of-service study；
- subsidy agreement；
- universal-service obligation；
- customer tariff / contract；
- meter / billing；
- service-quality data；
- outage / incident logs；
- maintenance / asset-condition reports；
- investment plan；
- government guarantees；
- fiscal-risk statement；
- renegotiation；
- step-in notice；
- insolvency；
- termination；
- handback inspection；
- regulator decision / appeal。

核心：

> **公共事業案件不能被「公營／民營」二分法吃掉；真正要打開的是誰擁有 asset、誰承擔 public-service responsibility、誰營運、誰定價、誰出錢、誰承擔 failure，以及 operator 消失後服務能不能活下來。**

The IMO Net-Zero Framework was approved but its adoption session was adjourned, so APPROVED != BINDING ADOPTED. Separately, the 2010 HNS Convention has met entry conditions but will enter into force only on 29 November 2027.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **IMO — Net-zero shipping talks to resume in 2026**
  - URL: https://www.imo.org/en/mediacentre/pressbriefings/pages/imo-net-zero-shipping-talks-to-resume-in-2026.aspx

- **IMO — HNS treaty to enter into force in 2027**
  - URL: https://www.imo.org/en/mediacentre/pressbriefings/pages/imo-hns-treaty-to-enter-into-force-in-2027-.aspx
  - Verified point: Entry-into-force conditions were met in May 2026 and the 2010 HNS Convention will enter into force on 29 November 2027.

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-002-MARITIME_ADMIRALTY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "海商法／海事法"
  english_name: "Maritime / Admiralty Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "高度跨境、高風險、高價值且涉及船舶、貨物、航程與多方角色的海上活動，如何在運送、責任、保險、救助、碰撞、共同海損與責任限制之間配置風險？"

  historical_reason:
    "海上商業具有跨境、多方、長距離、自然與航行風險集中、船舶與貨物價值高、損害可能一次擴散至多方等特性。普通契約與侵權法可以提供基礎，但若完全逐案處理，會降低航運風險與責任的可預測性，因此海商法發展出大量特殊制度與國際統一規則。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "完全賠償 vs 海事責任限制"
    - "契約自由 vs 強制性國際運送責任"
    - "貨主利益 vs 承運人風險可預測性"
    - "單一事故 vs 多方、多契約、多保險關係"
    - "船舶財產性 vs 航行與公共安全性"
    - "國內私法 vs 國際統一法"

  shortest_maxim:
    "海商案件第一眼不要問『誰弄壞東西』；先問『這是哪一種海事關係、誰用哪一個身分承擔哪一段海上風險』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 5.1 第一眼｜ROLE BEFORE LIABILITY

普通侵權直覺會問：

> 「誰造成損害？」

海商第一眼還要先問：

> **他是以什麼海事法律身分出現在這條鏈上？**

可能包括：

```text
SHIPOWNER
BAREBOAT / DEMISE CHARTERER
TIME CHARTERER
VOYAGE CHARTERER
CARRIER
CONTRACTUAL CARRIER
ACTUAL CARRIER
SHIPPER
CONSIGNEE
HOLDER OF BILL OF LADING
FREIGHT FORWARDER / NVOCC
MASTER / CREW
SALVOR
INSURER / P&I
PORT / TERMINAL OPERATOR
CARGO INTEREST
```

同一家公司可能同時具有不同角色；同一事故也可能同時產生契約、侵權、海事責任限制與保險問題。

因此：

> **先辨角色，再談責任。**

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 5.7 證據特徵

海商案件常見高價值材料：

- 提單／海運單；
- 傭船契約；
- 訂艙文件；
- 貨物裝箱與封條紀錄；
- 貨況、理貨與檢驗報告；
- 裝卸紀錄；
- 航海日誌；
- 船舶航行資料；
- 氣象海象資料；
- 船級／檢驗／適航相關資料；
- 貨損通知；
- 電子郵件與指示；
- 保單與 P&I 文件；
- 共同海損理算；
- 救助契約；
- 船舶登記；
- 船舶管理與實際控制資料。

關鍵：

> **海商案件的「誰負責」常藏在文件與角色鏈，而不只藏在事故畫面。**

> **法位提醒：以下直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置、地方制度、最新法規或動態名單，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 5.9 Positive Law Gate

以下必須現查：

1. 準據法與法院／仲裁管轄；
2. 相關國家是否適用何種貨物運送公約或國內制度；
3. 責任限制之制度、限額、計算單位與破限條件；
4. 海事時效／除斥期間／通知期間；
5. 船舶扣押與海事優先權；
6. 污染、旅客、人身、救助、共同海損等特殊制度；
7. 提單／傭船契約之選法、仲裁與條款併入效力；
8. 船東、承運人、實際承運人等角色定義。

---

## FAILURE_MODES

## 5.8 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "貨損 → 直接套普通買賣瑕疵擔保，不先區分買賣與運送。"
  - "船東 → 當然等於契約承運人。"
  - "提單上有名字 → 當然就是唯一責任主體。"
  - "貨物損失多少 → 當然賠多少，不查責任限制。"
  - "事故發生在海上 → 自動進海洋法／UNCLOS。"
  - "看到海事公約 → 不查法域是否締約、實施或採何版本。"
  - "把責任限制誤當成免責。"
  - "把海事優先權、船舶扣押與普通財產保全完全等同。"
```


UNECE WP.29 adopted a fully automated driving regulation in June 2026, but global technical-rule adoption does not itself answer domestic road-use permission, type approval, driver/operator liability or micromobility rules.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNECE — automated driving regulation press releases**
  - URL: https://unece.org/media/press?f%5B0%5D=program%3A295&f%5B1%5D=topics%3A1373
  - Verified point: UNECE WP.29 adopted the first global regulation for fully automated driving systems on 24 June 2026; domestic road-use and liability rules remain separate.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留共同母層／入口與本卡的穩定 first move；已由專門卡取得獨立處理資格的細節，不在母卡重新擴張成第二份 authoritative specialization。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-052-ROAD_TRAFFIC_MOBILITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "交通法"
  english_name: "Road Traffic / Mobility Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "在道路這個多人同時使用的共同空間裡，誰有資格駕駛何種車輛、車輛需符合哪些安全條件、行車優先與速度如何配置、國家如何執法；發生事故時，又如何把交通違規、行政責任、刑事責任、民事過失、保險與產品／道路缺陷分成不同法律軸？"

  historical_reason:
    "道路交通的核心問題不是單一駕駛人的自由，而是大量陌生人在高速度、高能量與有限空間下同時移動。若沒有一致號誌、駕照、車輛技術、道路規則與事故處理制度，任何人的自由都會變成他人的不可預測風險。交通法因此透過道路規則、駕駛資格、車輛安全、執法、保險與責任制度，把物理運動轉化為可預測秩序。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "移動自由 vs 公共安全"
    - "個人駕駛責任 vs 系統／道路／車輛責任"
    - "快速執法 vs 程序保障"
    - "一致交通規則 vs 地方道路條件"
    - "車輛創新 vs 型式認證／安全"
    - "自動駕駛效率 vs 人機責任分配"
    - "事故補償 vs 過失責任"
    - "交通監控 vs 隱私"
    - "道路使用公平 vs 弱勢道路使用者安全"
    - "跨境互認 vs 國內駕照／車籍主權"

  shortest_maxim:
    "交通法第一眼不是『誰撞誰』；先問『誰在什麼道路、以什麼車／身份、受到哪一條行車規則拘束、哪個危險如何形成，事故後行政、刑事、民事與保險各走哪一條』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 58.1 第一眼｜ROAD → USER → VEHICLE → AUTHORITY → RULE → CONDUCT → HAZARD → INCIDENT → LIABILITY AXES

```text
ROAD
＝公共道路？私人道路？高速公路？行人區？自行車道？

USER
＝駕駛？行人？騎士？乘客？施工人員？

VEHICLE
＝汽車？機車？自行車？電動載具？自動駕駛車？

AUTHORITY
＝駕照、車籍、營業資格、保險是否成立？

RULE
＝速度、號誌、路權、轉彎、超車、酒駕、手機？

CONDUCT
＝實際做了什麼？

HAZARD
＝視線、路況、車況、號誌、系統失效？

INCIDENT
＝碰撞？傷亡？財損？未遂危險？

LIABILITY AXES
＝行政？刑事？民事？保險？產品責任？道路管理？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 58.19 證據特徵

- licence / registration；
- insurance；
- traffic signal plan；
- CCTV；
- EDR / telemetry；
- GPS；
- phone logs；
- skid / damage；
- vehicle inspection；
- repair / recall；
- software logs；
- road maintenance；
- police scene diagram；
- toxicology；
- camera calibration；
- witness；
- accident reconstruction。

核心：

> **交通案真正要重建的是「事故前幾秒的物理世界＋當時有效的道路規則」，不是事後誰說自己看到什麼而已。**

## 58.21 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依事故地、車種與日期現查：

1. road traffic rules；
2. right of way；
3. speed limits；
4. licensing；
5. vehicle registration；
6. compulsory insurance；
7. DUI / drug driving；
8. distracted driving；
9. hit-and-run；
10. traffic offences；
11. accident reporting；
12. vehicle technical rules；
13. periodic inspection；
14. automated driving；
15. owner / operator liability；
16. weak-road-user duties；
17. road authority liability；
18. camera enforcement；
19. ADR where applicable；
20. Vienna / Geneva road conventions；
21. limitation / claims procedure。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 PHYSICAL_MOBILITY_LOGISTICS_FRONTIER 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-053-AVIATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "航空法"
  english_name: "Aviation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家如何在主權空域內允許航空器跨境、安全且可預測地飛行；航空器如何取得國籍、登記、適航與營運資格；航空公司、機場、空管與飛航人員如何分工；事故、旅客、貨物、保安、環境與航空器融資又如何進入專門的國際與國內法律制度？"

  historical_reason:
    "航空具有高速、跨境、高技術、高事故後果與全球網路特徵。若每個國家只用完全不同的飛航標準，國際民航無法安全運作；若只依國際技術規則又會忽略國家空域主權與執法。航空法因此形成 Chicago Convention、ICAO SARPs、雙邊航空協定、國內航空法、Montreal carrier-liability regime、aircraft-finance instruments 與安全／事故調查制度的多層架構。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "空域主權 vs 國際航空流通"
    - "全球技術一致性 vs 國內監管主權"
    - "航空安全 vs 商業效率"
    - "事故獨立調查 vs 刑事／民事追責"
    - "航空公司營運自由 vs passenger protection"
    - "carrier liability uniformity vs domestic tort law"
    - "航空保安 vs 隱私／自由"
    - "機場容量／slots vs 競爭"
    - "航空器融資可預測性 vs insolvency"
    - "國際航空成長 vs noise / climate obligations"
    - "無人機／RPAS 創新 vs 空域安全"

  shortest_maxim:
    "航空法第一眼不是『飛機在哪裡出事』；先問『哪個空域、哪個航空器、誰是 operator／carrier、依哪個國際／國內法源取得飛行資格、事故或運送落在哪一套專門責任制度』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 59.1 第一眼｜AIRSPACE → AIRCRAFT → STATE OF REGISTRY → OPERATOR → FLIGHT → SAFETY RULE → CARRIAGE → INCIDENT → LIABILITY / INVESTIGATION

```text
AIRSPACE
＝哪國領空？high seas？international route？

AIRCRAFT
＝civil aircraft？state aircraft？drone / RPAS？

STATE OF REGISTRY
＝航空器登記在哪國？

OPERATOR
＝誰實際營運？

FLIGHT
＝private？commercial？scheduled？charter？

SAFETY RULE
＝licensing、airworthiness、operations、ATC、aerodrome？

CARRIAGE
＝international carriage of passengers / baggage / cargo？

INCIDENT
＝delay？baggage loss？injury？crash？security event？

LEGAL PATH
＝Chicago / SARPs？Montreal 1999？domestic law？criminal law？insurance？
```

EU rail passenger rights are current; new capacity and telematics rules were adopted in 2026. A May 2026 single-ticket passenger package is still a proposal, so BUILD02 locks PROPOSAL != FINAL CURRENT LAW.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Rail passenger rights**
  - URL: https://transport.ec.europa.eu/transport-themes/passenger-rights/rail-passenger-rights_en
  - Verified point: Regulation (EU) 2021/782 applies from 7 June 2023 within its scope.
- **European Commission — new EU rail capacity rules**
  - URL: https://transport.ec.europa.eu/news-events/news/new-eu-rules-will-improve-cross-border-rail-traffic-and-optimise-network-capacity-2026-06-10_en
  - Verified point: New rail capacity rules were adopted in June 2026 with later operational timetable effects; adoption is not immediate completion of every implementation step.
- **European Commission — TSI Telematics 2026**
  - URL: https://transport.ec.europa.eu/news-events/news/commission-adopts-harmonised-eu-specifications-data-sharing-rail-transport-2026-02-10_en
  - Verified point: The Commission adopted harmonised rail data-sharing specifications in February 2026.
- **European Commission — 2026 single-ticket passenger proposal**

  - Verified point: The May 2026 single-ticket package is a proposal, not current final law.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 PHYSICAL_MOBILITY_LOGISTICS_FRONTIER 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-054-RAIL_SURFACE_TRANSPORT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "鐵路／運輸法"
  english_name: "Rail / Surface Transport Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "在鐵路與其他地面運輸網路中，基礎設施管理人、營運人、承運人、車輛所有人、旅客、貨主與監管者如何分工；國際旅客／貨物運送契約、安全、危險物、事故、網路接取與跨境責任又如何在不同法律層間分配？"

  historical_reason:
    "鐵路不是一台車在一條路上行駛，而是一個由軌道、號誌、調度、車站、列車、時刻、維修與邊境制度共同運作的網路。運輸私法又需要處理貨損、延誤與旅客傷害。國際鐵路法因此透過 COTIF 及其 CIV、CIM、RID 等附錄形成統一規則；道路貨運則有 CMR / e-CMR。運輸法的核心是把 infrastructure risk、operation risk 與 carriage liability 分開。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "基礎設施自然壟斷 vs 開放接取／競爭"
    - "網路安全 vs 營運效率"
    - "列車準點 vs 安全裕度"
    - "承運人契約責任 vs infrastructure responsibility"
    - "旅客保護 vs mass-transit standardization"
    - "貨物流通效率 vs carrier liability"
    - "dangerous goods 便利 vs 高後果風險"
    - "國際統一法 vs 國內鐵路監管"
    - "紙本運單 vs 電子運輸文件"
    - "單一運輸契約 vs multimodal segments"
    - "事故責任 vs independent safety investigation"

  shortest_maxim:
    "鐵路／運輸法第一眼不是『哪台列車／哪台卡車出了問題』；先問『誰控制基礎設施、誰實際承運、哪一段運輸、哪一份運送文件、哪個安全制度、損害發生在哪一段』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 60.1 第一眼｜MODE → INFRASTRUCTURE → OPERATOR → CARRIER → CONTRACT → VEHICLE → SAFETY → CARGO / PASSENGER → INCIDENT → LIABILITY

```text
MODE
＝rail？road freight？combined transport？

INFRASTRUCTURE
＝軌道／站場／號誌／道路／terminal 誰管理？

OPERATOR
＝誰控制列車／車隊？

CARRIER
＝誰是契約承運人？actual / substitute carrier？

CONTRACT
＝旅客票？CIM consignment note？CMR note？multimodal contract？

VEHICLE
＝wagon / locomotive / truck 技術資格？

SAFETY
＝traffic management、maintenance、dangerous goods？

CARGO / PASSENGER
＝貨損？延誤？旅客傷亡？行李？

INCIDENT
＝哪一段發生？

LIABILITY
＝carrier？infrastructure manager？vehicle keeper？shipper？third party？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 60.22 證據特徵

- infrastructure logs；
- signal data；
- dispatch records；
- train event recorder；
- speed；
- maintenance；
- wagon records；
- ticket；

- passenger records；

核心：

> **運輸案要先畫「哪個主體在什麼時間取得對人、貨、車與基礎設施的控制」，責任才不會在 logistics chain 裡漂移。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 PHYSICAL_MOBILITY_LOGISTICS_FRONTIER 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-087-POSTAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "郵政法"
  english_name: "Postal Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家如何確保 universal postal service，同時允許 courier / parcel / e-commerce competition；member country、designated operator、postal regulator、customs、air carrier、sender、addressee 與 private courier 各有何角色；跨境 mail / parcel 如何透過 UPU Acts、customs data、dangerous-goods、liability、remuneration、quality-of-service 與 security 被接成一條全球實體通信鏈？"

  historical_reason:
    "郵政是最早的全球通信網路之一。單一國家若沒有共同 address、dispatch、remuneration、customs、liability 與 transit rules，一封跨境信件會在每個邊境重新談判。UPU 因此把 member countries 與 designated operators 接成全球 postal territory / exchange framework；現代 postal law 又必須面對 courier competition、e-commerce parcels、electronic customs data、dangerous goods、postal financial services、digital substitution 與 universal-service sustainability。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "universal service vs competition"
    - "designated operator privileges vs market neutrality"
    - "affordable postage vs financial sustainability"
    - "postal secrecy vs customs / security inspection"
    - "fast delivery vs dangerous-goods control"
    - "sender declarations vs operator verification"
    - "cross-border treaty rules vs domestic consumer law"
    - "postal customs data vs privacy"
    - "liability limits vs full compensation"
    - "rural coverage vs cost efficiency"
    - "physical network vs digital substitution"
    - "public-service mission vs e-commerce logistics"
    - "State treaty obligation vs operator commercial autonomy"

  shortest_maxim:
    "郵政法第一眼不是『包裹交給郵局了』；先問『這是 UPU designated-operator mail、domestic post 還是 private courier；哪種 service、哪個 sender declaration、哪個 customs / dangerous-goods gate、何時交接、誰對 loss / delay / detention 負責』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 93.1 第一眼｜SERVICE → ACTOR → ACCEPTANCE → ITEM / CONTENT → ADDRESS / DATA → TRANSPORT → CUSTOMS → DELIVERY → LIABILITY → REMUNERATION → COMPLAINT

```text
SERVICE
＝letter post？parcel？EMS？registered？express courier？

ACTOR
＝member State？designated operator？private courier？air carrier？

ACCEPTANCE
＝item accepted under what terms / declaration?

ITEM / CONTENT
＝documents？goods？dangerous / prohibited item？

ADDRESS / DATA
＝address valid？electronic advance data complete？

TRANSPORT
＝surface？air？transit operator？

CUSTOMS
＝postal customs declaration？EAD？inspection？duty？

DELIVERY
＝attempt？signature？collection point？return?

LIABILITY
＝loss？damage？delay？force majeure？limit?

REMUNERATION
＝inter-operator dues / rates?

COMPLAINT
＝operator claim？regulator？consumer body？court？
```

The UN space-treaty architecture remains current, but party status is dynamic and national authorization/licensing, registration, debris, STM, resources, spectrum and orbital-use rules remain jurisdiction-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNOOSA — status of space treaties**
  - URL: https://www.unoosa.org/oosa/sk/ourwork/spacelaw/treaties/status/index.html
  - Verified point: Space-treaty party status is officially maintained and dynamic; treaty existence does not establish a State’s party status in a specific case.

依 INT-12 保留為 PHYSICAL_MOBILITY_LOGISTICS_FRONTIER 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-099-SPACE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "太空法"
  english_name: "Space Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項space activity由哪個State / private operator執行、是否涉及launch / procurement / territory / facility / registry等international connecting factors；Outer Space Treaty、Rescue Agreement、Liability Convention、Registration Convention、Moon Agreement與national space legislation如何分層；launch licence、frequency / orbital coordination、registration、remote sensing、debris、collision、space resources、human spaceflight與re-entry各需何種資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "space objects高速跨越國境且可長期留在orbit，傳統territorial sovereignty無法像航空法一樣逐段管理。1960年代起國際法因此把space freedom、non-appropriation、State responsibility、launching-State liability、registration、rescue與international cooperation接成制度。NewSpace之後private launch、mega-constellations、lunar activity、space resources與debris又使national authorization / supervision、space traffic coordination與sustainability成為新核心。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "freedom of exploration/use vs non-appropriation"
    - "private commercial activity vs State responsibility"
    - "launch innovation vs public / third-party risk"
    - "orbital use vs debris / congestion"
    - "national licensing vs globally shared environment"
    - "space resources vs non-appropriation"
    - "remote sensing freedom vs data / security concerns"
    - "registration transparency vs complex ownership"
    - "collision avoidance vs absence of global traffic authority"
    - "military / dual use vs peaceful-use principles"
    - "spectrum / orbit coordination vs commercial competition"
    - "human spaceflight freedom vs safety / rescue"
    - "planetary protection vs scientific / commercial missions"

  shortest_maxim:
    "太空法第一眼不是『東西上太空了』；先問『誰launch / procure / operate、從哪裡launch、登記在哪個State、誰有authorization / continuing supervision、使用哪個frequency / orbit、若碰撞／墜落由哪個launching State與operator承擔哪一層責任』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 105.1 第一眼｜ACTIVITY → STATE CONNECTION → OPERATOR → AUTHORIZATION → LAUNCHING STATE → REGISTRATION → ORBIT / SPECTRUM → OPERATION → DEBRIS / COLLISION → DAMAGE → RE-ENTRY / TERMINATION → REMEDY

```text
ACTIVITY
＝launch？satellite service？remote sensing？lunar mission？human spaceflight？

STATE CONNECTION
＝nationality？territory？facility？procured launch？registry?

OPERATOR
＝government agency？company？university？international organization?

AUTHORIZATION
＝launch licence？operator licence？payload approval?

LAUNCHING STATE
＝launches / procures launch / territory / facility?

REGISTRATION
＝national registry？UN registration?

ORBIT / SPECTRUM
＝LEO? GEO? lunar? ITU filing / assignment?

OPERATION
＝command / control？remote sensing？communications?

DEBRIS / COLLISION
＝conjunction？avoidance？end-of-life?

DAMAGE
＝Earth surface？aircraft？outer space?

RE-ENTRY / TERMINATION
＝controlled? uncontrolled? disposal?

REMEDY
＝State claim? contract? national liability? insurance?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 105.37 Evidence architecture

- launch contract；
- national licence；
- operator / owner structure；
- payload approval；
- State registry；
- UN registration；
- orbital elements；
- ITU filing；
- telemetry / command logs；
- conjunction messages；
- maneuver decisions；
- debris / re-entry tracking；
- insurance；
- damage evidence；
- space-resource mission plan；
- treaty status；
- Artemis participation；
- national legislation。

核心：

> **太空案不能被「誰擁有衛星」一句收掉；真正的責任鏈通常至少同時有operator、owner、launching State、State of registry、licensing State與frequency administration。**

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "100 km＝全球法定太空邊界。"
  - "outer space＝法律真空。"
  - "私人公司＝國家無國際責任。"
  - "launching State＝owner。"
  - "State of registry＝唯一launching State。"
  - "registration＝orbit title。"
  - "ITU filing＝軌道主權。"
  - "所有space damage＝absolute liability。"
  - "dead satellite＝無主物。"
  - "LTS Guidelines＝binding treaty。"
  - "Artemis Accords＝treaty。"
  - "national resource law＝全球space-resource property law。"
  - "Moon Agreement＝全球普遍規則。"
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "BIOTECH_LAW_MULTI_LAYER_DYNAMIC__CARTAGENA_PROTOCOL_CURRENT_FOR_PARTIES__WHO_GENOME_EDITING_FRAMEWORK_NON_BINDING__EU_BIOTECH_ACT_MATERIAL_REMAINS_PROPOSAL_NOT_OPERATIVE_REPLACEMENT"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The Cartagena Protocol is current for its Parties, while WHO genome-editing materials are non-binding guidance and EU Biotech Act material remains proposal-stage. BUILD02 therefore separates treaty law, domestic biosafety and policy/proposal material.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **Convention on Biological Diversity — Cartagena Protocol**
  - URL: https://bch.cbd.int/protocol?lg=0
  - Verified point: The Cartagena Protocol is in force for its Parties; party and domestic biosafety implementation must be checked.
- **WHO — Human genome editing recommendations**
  - URL: https://www.who.int/publications/i/item/9789240030060
  - Verified point: WHO genome-editing governance materials are recommendations/guidance, not self-executing positive law.
- **European Commission — European Biotech Act**
  - URL: https://health.ec.europa.eu/biotechnology/european-biotech-act_en
  - Verified point: Current European Biotech Act materials are proposal-stage policy/legislative material and are not assumed to have replaced operative sector law.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 BIOLOGICAL_PRODUCT_SAFETY_LIFECYCLE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-026-BIOTECHNOLOGY_BIOSCIENCES_LAW"
  chinese_name: "生技法／生命科學法"
  english_name: "Biotechnology / Biosciences Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當生命科學能夠讀取、保存、培養、改造、複製與跨境移動基因、細胞、生物材料與生物體時，法律如何在研究自由、人體尊嚴、生物安全、遺傳隱私、商業化、資源取得與社會風險之間設定實驗前與利用前的資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "生技活動的結果可能不可逆、可遺傳、可繁殖或跨境擴散；人類生物材料又同時具有人格、資料、研究與商業價值。普通契約與物權概念無法充分處理基因編輯、胚胎研究、biobank、LMO、基因資料與遺傳資源利益分享。因此生技法是研究倫理、人權、環境、生物安全、資料、IP 與產品監管的交界領域。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "科學自由 vs 人體尊嚴與研究參與者保護"
    - "研究創新 vs 未知／不可逆生物風險"
    - "個人同意 vs 群體／家族遺傳影響"
    - "人類生物材料利用 vs 商品化界線"
    - "基因資料共享 vs 隱私與再識別"
    - "企業研發投資 vs 遺傳資源與傳統知識利益分享"
    - "基因改造效益 vs 生態擴散"
    - "專利激勵 vs 研究與醫療可近性"
    - "跨境研究 vs 法域、倫理與生物安全差異"

  shortest_maxim:
    "生技法第一眼不是『技術能不能做到』；先問『改的是什麼生命材料、對誰／哪個生態系統產生不可逆影響、誰能同意、誰能放行、誰取得利益、失控時誰負責』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 32.1 第一眼｜MATERIAL → INTERVENTION → HUMAN / ENVIRONMENT → CONSENT → BIOSAFETY → DATA → BENEFIT → PRODUCT

```text
MATERIAL
＝DNA？細胞？組織？胚胎？gamete？微生物？LMO？植物／動物？biobank sample？

INTERVENTION
＝測序？培養？基因編輯？複製？移植？釋放？合成？

TARGET / EXPOSURE
＝研究參與者？後代？家族？社區？環境？

CONSENT / AUTHORITY
＝誰提供？誰同意？倫理委員會？主管機關？

BIOSAFETY
＝contained use？field release？transboundary movement？

DATA
＝genetic data？sequence data？health data？

BENEFIT
＝專利？產品？資料？利益分享？

PRODUCT
＝最後是否成為藥品、器材、食品、農業產品？
```

- protocol；
- ethics approval；
- consent；
- sample provenance；
- chain of custody；
- biobank governance；
- sequencing logs；
- data access；
- biosafety classification；

- field release plan；
- risk assessment；
- genetic construct；
- off-target analysis；

核心：

## 32.14 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域、材料與用途現查：

1. 人體研究；
2. research ethics committee；
3. informed consent；
4. human tissue / cells；
5. embryos / gametes；
6. stem cells；
7. somatic gene therapy；
8. heritable genome editing；
9. genetic testing；
10. genetic privacy / discrimination；
11. biobanks；
12. genetic data；
13. LMO/GMO biosafety；
14. contained use / field release；
15. Cartagena Protocol Party / implementation；
16. Nagoya ABS；
17. traditional knowledge；
18. DSI / benefit-sharing；
19. dual-use / biosecurity；
20. biotech patent；
21. clinical product regulation。

Existing EU authorization/safety/efficacy frameworks remain the baseline. Pharmaceutical reform and medical-device simplification material may be politically or legislatively advanced, but BUILD02 does not treat proposal/agreement text as operative law without final text and application dates.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — Reform of EU pharmaceutical legislation**
  - URL: https://health.ec.europa.eu/medicinal-products/reform-eu-pharmaceutical-legislation_en

- **European Commission — Medical devices regulatory overview**
  - URL: https://health.ec.europa.eu/medical-devices-new-regulations/overview_en
  - Verified point: Existing MDR/IVDR rules remain the operative baseline while simplification proposals must not be treated as final law before adoption/application.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

依 INT-12 保留為 BIOLOGICAL_PRODUCT_SAFETY_LIFECYCLE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-027-FOOD_DRUG_MEDICAL_PRODUCTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "食品藥物法"
  english_name: "Food & Drug / Medical Products Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當食品與醫療產品會被大量製造、跨境流通並直接進入人體，而一般消費者無法自行驗證成分、製程、安全、有效性與品質時，國家如何把產品生命週期轉化成上市前證據、製造品質、標示、上市後監測、召回與責任制度？"

  historical_reason:
    "藥品、生物製劑與醫療器材具有高度科學複雜性，患者無法自行驗證品質與療效；食品則具有大規模供應鏈、污染與累積風險。單靠產品責任等到傷害後賠償成本過高，因此食品藥物法以風險為基礎，在產品進入市場前建立許可／標準，在上市後持續監測不良事件、製造品質、標示與召回。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "快速取得治療 vs 完整安全／有效性證據"
    - "創新藥研發成本 vs 藥品可近性"
    - "上市前試驗 vs 真實世界未知風險"
    - "產品資訊透明 vs 商業秘密"
    - "監管一致性 vs 緊急公共衛生彈性"
    - "食品產業效率 vs 食品安全"
    - "預防性召回 vs 不必要恐慌／浪費"
    - "企業自主管理 vs 政府市場監測"
    - "全球供應鏈 vs 國家監管能力"
    - "科學證據更新 vs 核准決定穩定性"

  shortest_maxim:
    "食品藥物法第一眼不是『這產品有沒有害人』；先問『它是什麼法定產品、能不能上市、憑什麼證據上市、誰製造、怎麼標示、上市後出現什麼新風險、誰有權召回或限制』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 33.1 第一眼｜CLASSIFICATION → AUTHORIZATION → EVIDENCE → MANUFACTURE → LABEL → MARKET → SIGNAL → ACTION

```text
CLASSIFICATION
＝食品？藥品？生物製劑？醫療器材？化粧品？保健／補充產品？組合產品？

AUTHORIZATION
＝需核准？註冊？許可？通報？豁免？

EVIDENCE
＝品質、安全、有效性／性能要什麼資料？

MANUFACTURE
＝GMP／QMS／HACCP？哪個工廠？

LABEL / CLAIM
＝成分、適應症、風險、警語、營養、健康宣稱？

MARKET
＝誰進口、分銷、處方、銷售？

SIGNAL
＝不良事件？污染？品質缺陷？falsified product？

ACTION
＝label change？warning？recall？suspension？withdrawal？
```

```yaml

  qualified_status: "PRODUCT_SAFETY_MARKET_SURVEILLANCE_CURRENT__EU_GPSR_APPLIES_FROM_2024_12_13__CRA_REPORTING_BEGINS_2026_09_11_AND_MAIN_PRODUCT_CYBER_RULES_2027_12_11__SECTOR_PRODUCT_RULES_REQUIRED"
```

The EU GPSR applies from 13 December 2024. CRA reporting begins 11 September 2026 and main cyber-product obligations in December 2027; product-safety, market-surveillance and sector-specific regimes therefore require lifecycle/date routing.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **European Commission — General Product Safety Regulation implementation materials**
  - URL: https://commission.europa.eu/business-economy-euro/product-safety-and-requirements/product-safety/general-product-safety-regulation_en
  - Verified point: The General Product Safety Regulation applies from 13 December 2024 within its scope; sector product regimes still require separate routing.
- **European Commission — Cyber Resilience Act summary**
  - URL: https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/cra-summary
  - Verified point: CRA reporting obligations begin 11 September 2026 and the main obligations apply from 11 December 2027; entry into force is not full current applicability.


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留為跨域 horizontal overlay；只在本卡條件被案件觸發時疊加，不取代案件原本的 primary substantive route。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-091-PRODUCT_SAFETY_MARKET_SURVEILLANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "產品安全／市場監督法"
  english_name: "Product Safety / Market Surveillance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一項 consumer product 從 design、material、manufacture、software、import、distribution、online listing、sale、use、repair / update 到 end-of-life，何時被法律視為 safe / unsafe；誰負 lifecycle safety、traceability、warning、incident-reporting、stop-sale、corrective action / recall義務，market-surveillance authority 又憑何種risk / evidence介入？"

  historical_reason:
    "mass production、global supply chains與e-commerce讓一個design flaw可以同時傷害數百萬消費者。單靠事故後tort不足以阻止危險商品持續流通，因此現代 product-safety law 將責任前移：design risk、technical requirements、standards、conformity assessment、traceability、injury data、market surveillance、rapid alerts、recalls與cross-border cooperation共同構成 preventive system。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "innovation / market access vs preventive safety"
    - "mandatory rules vs voluntary standards"
    - "manufacturer responsibility vs supply-chain fragmentation"
    - "fast e-commerce vs traceability"
    - "risk uncertainty vs precautionary intervention"
    - "recall speed vs due process / business loss"
    - "global product design vs national requirements"
    - "consumer misuse vs foreseeable misuse"
    - "software updates vs physical-product lifecycle"
    - "second-hand / refurbished markets vs original compliance"
    - "safety transparency vs trade secret"
    - "vulnerable users vs average-user assumptions"
    - "product safety vs sustainability / repairability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "產品安全法第一眼不是『產品有沒有認證』；先問『產品是什麼、誰設計／製造／進口／上架、危險在哪個lifecycle stage形成、哪些mandatory rules與standards適用、market data顯示什麼、誰現在有能力stop sale / recall / warn』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 97.1 第一眼｜PRODUCT → USER / FORESEEABLE USE → ECONOMIC OPERATOR → HAZARD → REQUIREMENT / STANDARD → CONFORMITY → TRACEABILITY → MARKET DATA → RISK ASSESSMENT → CORRECTIVE ACTION → LIABILITY / REVIEW

```text
PRODUCT
＝toy？appliance？furniture？connected device？battery？second-hand item？

USER / USE
＝adult？child？older person？disabled user？foreseeable misuse？

ECONOMIC OPERATOR
＝manufacturer？brand owner？importer？distributor？retailer？platform？

HAZARD
＝mechanical？electrical？chemical？thermal？fire？choking？cyber-physical？

REQUIREMENT / STANDARD
＝mandatory technical rule？voluntary standard？sector code？

CONFORMITY
＝test？declaration？certification？mark？

TRACEABILITY
＝batch？serial？supplier？importer？buyer records？

MARKET DATA
＝complaints？injuries？near misses？recalls elsewhere？

RISK ASSESSMENT
＝probability × severity × exposure × vulnerable users？

CORRECTIVE ACTION
＝warning？repair？software update？withdrawal？recall？destruction？

LIABILITY / REVIEW
＝regulatory appeal？consumer claim？tort？criminal?
```

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置、地方制度、最新法規或動態名單，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 97.34 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依 product、market、actor、date 現查：

1. general / sector product-safety statute；
2. mandatory technical requirements；
3. applicable standards；
4. conformity presumption；
5. testing / certification；
6. accreditation；
7. conformity mark；
8. manufacturer duties；
9. importer duties；
10. distributor / retailer duties；
11. online marketplace duties；
12. traceability；
13. labelling / warnings；
14. software / updates；
15. vulnerable consumers；
16. incident reporting；
17. market surveillance；
18. sampling / testing；
19. stop-sale / withdrawal；
20. recall；
21. recall effectiveness；
22. cross-border alerts；
23. second-hand / refurbished goods；
24. customs cooperation；
25. product liability；
26. warranty / consumer remedies；
27. criminal / administrative offences；
28. limitation / records；
29. appeals / judicial review；
30. UN 2025 Principles / OECD 2020 / UNECE resources only at their proper soft-law level。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "CULTURAL_HERITAGE_TREATY_FRAMEWORK_CURRENT__UNESCO_1970_AND_1954_PARTY_STATUS_CURRENTLY_MAINTAINED__EXPORT_RESTITUTION_OWNERSHIP_INDIGENOUS_AND_DOMESTIC_PROTECTION_RULES_DYNAMIC"
  verification_scope: "current international/EU framework and transition status where a transnational source exists; domestic merits, classification, permits, remedies and case-specific applicability not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The UNESCO 1970 and 1954 cultural-property frameworks remain current with maintained party-status records. Export, restitution/return, ownership, wartime protection and indigenous/community claims still depend on treaty scope and domestic law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNESCO — 1970 Convention**
  - URL: https://www.unesco.org/en/legal-affairs/convention-means-prohibiting-and-preventing-illicit-import-export-and-transfer-ownership-cultural?hub=66775
  - Verified point: UNESCO maintains current party/status and basic texts for the 1970 Convention; return/export effects remain treaty- and domestic-law specific.
- **UNESCO — 1954 Hague Convention States Parties**
  - URL: https://www.unesco.org/en/heritage-armed-conflicts/states-parties?hub=415
  - Verified point: UNESCO maintains current State-party status for the 1954 Hague cultural-property framework.

依 INT-12 保留為 CULTURAL_PHYSICAL_HERITAGE 內的專門節點；本卡承擔自身 first move、證據／程序與 positive-law gate，跨主體問題交回 CROSS_DLM_ROUTING。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-073-CULTURAL_PROPERTY_HERITAGE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "文化資產／文化遺產法"
  english_name: "Cultural Property / Cultural Heritage Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個物件、建築、遺址、景觀、考古材料、傳統、技藝、儀式或文化空間如何取得文化遺產法律位置；owner、State、community、museum、dealer、researcher、developer 與 military actor 各有何權利義務；保存、改造、發掘、出口、交易、借展、戰爭保護、返還與數位利用如何分層？"

  historical_reason:
    "文化遺產同時具有財產、歷史、身份、社群記憶、學術與公共利益。單靠一般財產法容易把古蹟、考古物與傳統知識當成普通可交易物；反過來，單靠『文化重要』又容易讓國家取得無限控制。文化資產法因此建立 identification、designation、safeguarding、conservation、archaeological control、export/import、provenance、community participation、armed-conflict protection、restitution 與 sustainable-use 等制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "私人所有權 vs 文化公共利益"
    - "保存 vs 開發／居住／使用"
    - "國家文化政策 vs community self-definition"
    - "學術研究 vs sacred / sensitive heritage"
    - "博物館公共展示 vs provenance / restitution"
    - "文物市場流通 vs 防止非法交易"
    - "考古發掘 vs 原址保存"
    - "旅遊利用 vs authenticity / community life"
    - "國家認證 vs living heritage dynamism"
    - "armed-conflict military operations vs heritage protection"
    - "數位保存／再現 vs IP / personality / sacred restrictions"
    - "返還正義 vs private title / limitation / jurisdiction"

  shortest_maxim:
    "文化資產法第一眼不是『這東西很珍貴』；先問『它是什麼類型的 heritage、誰依法識別／指定、誰擁有／保管／與它有文化關係、哪一個行為會改變它、需要誰同意、能不能移動／出口／交易／返還』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 79.1 第一眼｜HERITAGE TYPE → LEGAL STATUS → OWNER / CUSTODIAN → COMMUNITY → LOCATION / ORIGIN → PROPOSED ACT → PERMIT / DUTY → PROVENANCE → REMEDY

```text
HERITAGE TYPE
＝monument？site？archaeological object？museum object？intangible practice？sacred item？

LEGAL STATUS
＝World Heritage？national monument？inventory only？undesignated heritage？

OWNER / CUSTODIAN
＝private owner？State？museum？religious body？community？

COMMUNITY
＝誰實際承載、實踐、認同這項 heritage？

LOCATION / ORIGIN
＝原址？出土地？戰爭／占領區？跨境？

PROPOSED ACT
＝repair？demolish？excavate？sell？export？digitize？perform？

PERMIT / DUTY
＝heritage permit？archaeological licence？export certificate？

PROVENANCE
＝來源與 title chain 是否完整？

REMEDY
＝injunction？restoration？return？restitution？compensation？criminal / admin sanction？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 79.26 證據特徵

- designation / inventory；
- cadastral / title；
- nomination file；
- management plan；
- conservation report；
- archaeological context；
- excavation permit；
- provenance；
- export / import certificate；
- customs；
- dealer / auction record；
- museum accession；
- theft database；
- wartime / occupation records；
- community consultation；
- oral history；
- heritage impact assessment；
- restoration documentation；
- digital scan / rights terms；
- restitution request。

核心：

> **文化資產案不能只問「它現在在哪裡」；要追「它是什麼、何時取得何種法律地位、從哪裡來、如何離開原位置、誰作過哪些不可逆決定」。**

|---|---|---|
| `DNA` | European Commission — Digital Networks Act — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/digital-networks-act | The Commission adopted the Digital Networks Act proposal in January 2026; proposal status is not an operative replacement of the EECC. |
| `EUDI` | European Commission — European Digital Identity Regulation — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/eudi-regulation | The amended European Digital Identity framework is in force and Member States are to provide EU Digital Identity Wallets by the end of 2026. |
| `DATA_ACT` | European Commission — Data Act — https://commission.europa.eu/news-and-media/news/data-act-enters-force-what-it-means-you-2024-01-11_en | The Data Act became applicable on 12 September 2025; sector, personal-data and later amendment questions remain separate. |
| `ADEQUACY` | European Commission — Adequacy decisions — https://commission.europa.eu/law/law-topic/data-protection/international-dimension-data-protection/adequacy-decisions_en | Adequacy decisions are an official and changeable transfer-status list; transfer legality cannot be frozen generically. |
| `EMFA` | European Commission — European Media Freedom Act — https://commission.europa.eu/topics/democracy/eu-actions-protect-democracy/european-media-freedom-act_en | EMFA entered into force in 2024 and most provisions became applicable on 8 August 2025, subject to its phased provisions and domestic implementation interfaces. |
| `DSA` | European Commission — Digital Services Act Q&A — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/faqs/digital-services-act-questions-and-answers | The DSA generally applies from 17 February 2024; VLOP/VLOSE designation and enforcement status remain dynamic. |
| `AI50` | European Commission — AI Act Article 50 transparency guidelines — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/library/guidelines-transparency-obligations-providers-and-deployers-ai-systems | Article 50 transparency duties apply from 2 August 2026 within their scope; this does not collapse copyright, personality or contract analysis. |
| `GRATK` | WIPO Lex — Treaty on Intellectual Property, Genetic Resources and Associated Traditional Knowledge status — https://www.wipo.int/wipolex/en/treaties/ShowResults?search_what=N&treaty_id=19830 | The treaty is not yet in force; WIPO status records four ratification/accession notifications through 8 July 2026. |
| `GRATK_FAQ` | WIPO — GRATK Treaty FAQs — https://www.wipo.int/export/sites/www/tk/en/docs/faqs-gratk-treaty.pdf | Entry into force requires 15 instruments of ratification or accession and occurs three months after the fifteenth instrument. |
| `RDLT` | WIPO — Riyadh Design Law Treaty status document — https://www.wipo.int/documents/d/treaties/docs-en-rdlt.pdf | The Riyadh Design Law Treaty remained not yet in force in the current WIPO status material checked for 2026. |
| `NIS2` | European Commission — NIS2 infringement/referral update — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/news/commission-refers-ireland-spain-france-and-netherlands-court-justice-failing-transpose-rules | The 17 October 2024 transposition deadline has passed, but the Commission still reported incomplete transposition in several Member States in July 2026. |
| `CRA` | European Commission — Cyber Resilience Act summary — https://digital-strategy.ec.europa.eu/en/policies/cra-summary | CRA reporting obligations begin 11 September 2026 and the main obligations apply from 11 December 2027; entry into force is not full current applicability. |
| `NATURE` | European Commission — Nature Restoration Regulation — https://environment.ec.europa.eu/topics/nature-and-biodiversity/nature-restoration-regulation_en | The Regulation is in force; draft national restoration plans are due by 1 September 2026. |
| `CBAM` | European Commission — CBAM definitive regime — https://taxation-customs.ec.europa.eu/carbon-border-adjustment-mechanism/cbam-definitive-regime_en | The definitive CBAM regime applies from 1 January 2026 within its current scope and thresholds. |
| `CAP` | European Commission — CAP and environment — https://agriculture.ec.europa.eu/cap-my-country/sustainability/environmental-sustainability/cap-and-environment_en | The CAP 2023–27 framework is current; concrete subsidy, conditionality and national strategic-plan rules remain scheme and jurisdiction specific. |
| `EUDR` | European Commission — Regulation on deforestation-free products — https://environment.ec.europa.eu/topics/forests/deforestation/regulation-deforestation-free-products_en | EUDR application is deferred to 30 December 2026 for large/medium operators and 30 June 2027 for micro/small operators, subject to the official scope rules. |
| `FISH_TAC` | European Commission — TACs and quotas 2026 — https://oceans-and-fisheries.ec.europa.eu/fisheries-management/fishing-quotas/tacs-and-quotas-2026_en | 2026 fishing opportunities are fixed by stock/area and include regulations and amendments for 2026, 2027 and 2028; they are inherently date- and stock-specific. |
| `FISH_CONTROL` | European Commission — Fisheries Control Regulation — https://oceans-and-fisheries.ec.europa.eu/fisheries-management/enforcing-rules/control-regulation_en | Some revised control rules apply in 2026 while others apply only in 2028; CATCH import certification became mandatory from 10 January 2026. |
| `CRMA` | European Commission — Critical Raw Materials Act strategic projects — https://single-market-economy.ec.europa.eu/calls-expression-interest/call-applications-strategic-projects-under-critical-raw-materials-act_en | CRMA is current and strategic-project lists/calls are dynamic; project status does not substitute for domestic mining permits and closure duties. |
| `CITES` | CITES — official species checklist/index — https://cites.org/sites/default/files/eng/resources/pub/checklist11/CITES_species_index.pdf | CITES listings are maintained and changeable; species/listing status must be checked at the relevant date. |
| `ENERGY_H2` | European Commission — Hydrogen and decarbonised gas market — https://energy.ec.europa.eu/topics/markets-and-consumers/hydrogen-and-decarbonised-gas-market_en | The 2024 gas/hydrogen reform entered into force and Member States had a 5 August 2026 transposition deadline; deadline passage does not prove uniform domestic implementation. |
| `IMO_NZ` | IMO — Net-zero shipping talks to resume in 2026 — https://www.imo.org/en/mediacentre/pressbriefings/pages/imo-net-zero-shipping-talks-to-resume-in-2026.aspx | The IMO Net-Zero Framework had been approved but the adoption session was adjourned; approval is not binding adoption. |
| `IMO_HNS` | IMO — HNS treaty to enter into force in 2027 — https://www.imo.org/en/mediacentre/pressbriefings/pages/imo-hns-treaty-to-enter-into-force-in-2027-.aspx | Entry-into-force conditions were met in May 2026 and the 2010 HNS Convention will enter into force on 29 November 2027. |
| `UNECE_ADS` | UNECE — automated driving regulation press releases — https://unece.org/media/press?f%5B0%5D=program%3A295&f%5B1%5D=topics%3A1373 | UNECE WP.29 adopted the first global regulation for fully automated driving systems on 24 June 2026; domestic road-use and liability rules remain separate. |
| `EASA_UAS` | EASA — Easy Access Rules for Unmanned Aircraft Systems — https://www.easa.europa.eu/en/document-library/easy-access-rules/easy-access-rules-unmanned-aircraft-systems | EASA UAS rules were updated in 2026; aviation operation, licensing and State implementation remain multi-layered. |
| `RAIL_RIGHTS` | European Commission — Rail passenger rights — https://transport.ec.europa.eu/transport-themes/passenger-rights/rail-passenger-rights_en | Regulation (EU) 2021/782 applies from 7 June 2023 within its scope. |
| `RAIL_CAP` | European Commission — new EU rail capacity rules — https://transport.ec.europa.eu/news-events/news/new-eu-rules-will-improve-cross-border-rail-traffic-and-optimise-network-capacity-2026-06-10_en | New rail capacity rules were adopted in June 2026 with later operational timetable effects; adoption is not immediate completion of every implementation step. |
| `RAIL_TSI` | European Commission — TSI Telematics 2026 — https://transport.ec.europa.eu/news-events/news/commission-adopts-harmonised-eu-specifications-data-sharing-rail-transport-2026-02-10_en | The Commission adopted harmonised rail data-sharing specifications in February 2026. |
| `RAIL_PROP` | European Commission — 2026 single-ticket passenger proposal — https://transport.ec.europa.eu/document/download/fc019827-59c9-400f-a530-4d7d21957a7d_en?filename=COM_2026_233_Proposal_on_the_protection_of_passengers_with_single_tickets.pdf | The May 2026 single-ticket package is a proposal, not current final law. |
| `UPU` | Universal Postal Union — Acts — https://www.upu.int/en/Universal-Postal-Union/About-UPU/Acts | UPU publishes the Acts and decisions of the 2025 Dubai Congress; effect depends on the relevant Act, date and domestic/international implementation. |
| `UNOOSA` | UNOOSA — status of space treaties — https://www.unoosa.org/oosa/sk/ourwork/spacelaw/treaties/status/index.html | Space-treaty party status is officially maintained and dynamic; treaty existence does not establish a State’s party status in a specific case. |
| `CARTAGENA` | Convention on Biological Diversity — Cartagena Protocol — https://bch.cbd.int/protocol?lg=0 | The Cartagena Protocol is in force for its Parties; party and domestic biosafety implementation must be checked. |
| `WHO_HGE` | WHO — Human genome editing recommendations — https://www.who.int/publications/i/item/9789240030060 | WHO genome-editing governance materials are recommendations/guidance, not self-executing positive law. |
| `EU_BIOTECH` | European Commission — European Biotech Act — https://health.ec.europa.eu/biotechnology/european-biotech-act_en | Current European Biotech Act materials are proposal-stage policy/legislative material and are not assumed to have replaced operative sector law. |
| `EU_PHARMA` | European Commission — Reform of EU pharmaceutical legislation — https://health.ec.europa.eu/medicinal-products/reform-eu-pharmaceutical-legislation_en | The pharmaceutical reform has progressed politically, but final operative text, application dates and transitions must be checked rather than inferred from political agreement. |
| `EU_DEVICES` | European Commission — Medical devices regulatory overview — https://health.ec.europa.eu/medical-devices-new-regulations/overview_en | Existing MDR/IVDR rules remain the operative baseline while simplification proposals must not be treated as final law before adoption/application. |
| `GPSR` | European Commission — General Product Safety Regulation implementation materials — https://commission.europa.eu/business-economy-euro/product-safety-and-requirements/product-safety/general-product-safety-regulation_en | The General Product Safety Regulation applies from 13 December 2024 within its scope; sector product regimes still require separate routing. |
| `UNESCO1970` | UNESCO — 1970 Convention — https://www.unesco.org/en/legal-affairs/convention-means-prohibiting-and-preventing-illicit-import-export-and-transfer-ownership-cultural?hub=66775 | UNESCO maintains current party/status and basic texts for the 1970 Convention; return/export effects remain treaty- and domestic-law specific. |
| `UNESCO1954` | UNESCO — 1954 Hague Convention States Parties — https://www.unesco.org/en/heritage-armed-conflicts/states-parties?hub=415 | UNESCO maintains current State-party status for the 1954 Hague cultural-property framework. |

## BUILD02 的閉合資格

```text
41 REFRESH_REQUIRED
→ 41 QUALIFIED CURRENT-LAW STATUS

但：

QUALIFIED STATUS PASS
!= EVERY JURISDICTION VERIFIED
!= ENTRY INTO FORCE == FULL APPLICATION
!= TRANSPOSITION DEADLINE == COMPLETE IMPLEMENTATION
!= PROPOSAL / APPROVAL == OPERATIVE FINAL LAW
!= TREATY EXISTS == THIS STATE IS BOUND IN THIS CASE
!= DYNAMIC LIST / DESIGNATION == STATIC FOREVER
```

DB4 現在取得：

```text
V1.0 DB4 RELEASE-CANDIDATE QUALIFICATION
FOR STABLE METHOD + CURRENT FRAMEWORK / TRANSITION ROUTING

具體案件仍須重新查：
```
